        local clicked = cap and ui.leftReleased and hot

        if selected then
            Theme.drawRoundedRect(3, cur.x, cur.y, sw, sh, Theme.C.bgSelectionRow)
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.accentBright)
            render.drawRectFast(cur.x, cur.y + 3, 2, sh - 6)
        elseif hot then
            Theme.drawRoundedRect(3, cur.x, cur.y, sw, sh, Theme.C.bgControlHover, 140)
        end

        Theme.setFont(selected and Theme.Fonts.BodyBold or Theme.Fonts.Body)
        Theme.setColor(selected and Theme.C.textPrimary or Theme.C.textSecondary)
        render.drawSimpleText(cur.x + 8, cur.y + sh * 0.5, disp, TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)

        advanceCursor(cur, sw, sh)
        return clicked
    end

    function ImGui.RadioButton(label, active)
        local cur = currentCursor()
        if not cur then return false end
        local ui = ImGui._ui
        local disp = stripIdSuffix(label)
        Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Body)
        local tw = render.getTextSize(disp) or 30
        local rw = 22 + tw + 4
        local rh = 20
        local wid = ImGui.GetID("rad_" .. label)

        local hot, cap = ui:hitTest(wid, cur.x, cur.y, rw, rh)
        if hot and ui.leftPressed then ui:capture(wid) end
        local clicked = cap and ui.leftReleased and hot

        local cx, cy = cur.x + 9, cur.y + rh * 0.5
        Theme.drawFilledCircle(cx, cy, 7, hot and Theme.C.bgControlHover or Theme.C.bgInput)
        Theme.setColor((active or hot) and Theme.C.accent or Theme.C.borderLight)
        render.drawCircle(cx, cy, 7)
        if active then
            Theme.drawFilledCircle(cx, cy, 4, Theme.C.accentBright)
        end

        Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Body)
        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textPrimary)
        render.drawSimpleText(cur.x + 22, cy, disp, TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)

        advanceCursor(cur, rw, rh)
        return clicked
    end

    function ImGui.Checkbox(label, checked)
        local cur = currentCursor()
        if not cur then return checked, false end
        local ui = ImGui._ui
        local disp = stripIdSuffix(label)
        Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Body)
        local tw = render.getTextSize(disp) or 40
        local boxSize = 16
        local rowH = 20
        local totalW = boxSize + 7 + tw + 4
        local wid = ImGui.GetID("chk_" .. label)

        local hot, cap = ui:hitTest(wid, cur.x, cur.y, totalW, rowH)
        if hot and ui.leftPressed then ui:capture(wid) end
        local clicked = cap and ui.leftReleased and hot
        local nextVal = checked == true
        if clicked then nextVal = not nextVal end

        local bx, by = cur.x, cur.y + 2
        local r = math.min(4, Theme.Style.frameRounding or 4)
        local bgCol = nextVal and Theme.C.accentSoft or (hot and Theme.C.bgControlHover or Theme.C.bgInput)
        Theme.drawRoundedRect(r, bx, by, boxSize, boxSize, bgCol)
        Theme.setColor(nextVal and Theme.C.accentBright or (hot and Theme.C.borderFocus or Theme.C.borderLight))
        render.drawRectOutline(bx, by, boxSize, boxSize, 1)

        if nextVal then
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.accentBright)
            Theme.drawIcon("check", bx + 2, by + 2, 12)
        end

        Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Body)
        Theme.setColor(hot and Theme.C.textPrimary or Theme.C.textSecondary)
        render.drawSimpleText(bx + boxSize + 7, cur.y + rowH * 0.5, disp, TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)

        advanceCursor(cur, totalW, rowH)
        return nextVal, clicked
    end

    function ImGui.SliderFloat(label, val, minV, maxV, fmt, w)
        local cur = currentCursor()
        if not cur then return val, false end
        local ui = ImGui._ui
        local disp = stripIdSuffix(label)
        local totalW = (w and w > 0) and w or getItemWidth(cur)
        local rowH = 22
        local wid = ImGui.GetID("sldr_" .. label)

        -- Compact label-left / slider-right ImGui-style row when label is non-empty
        local labelW = (disp ~= "") and math.min(math.floor(totalW * 0.42), 130) or 0
        local trackX = cur.x + labelW
        local trackW = math.max(50, totalW - labelW)

        if disp ~= "" then
            Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Body)
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textSecondary)
            render.drawSimpleText(cur.x, cur.y + rowH * 0.5, disp, TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)
        end

        local hot, cap = ui:hitTest(wid, trackX, cur.y, trackW, rowH)
        if hot and ui.leftPressed then
            ui:capture(wid)
        end

        local newV = tonumber(val) or minV
        local changed = false
        if cap and ui.leftDown then
            local frac = math.clamp((ui.mx - trackX) / math.max(1, trackW), 0, 1)
            local raw = math.clamp(minV + (maxV - minV) * frac, minV, maxV)
            if math.abs(raw - newV) > 1e-6 then
                newV = raw
                changed = true
            end
        end

        local r = Theme.Style.frameRounding or 4
        Theme.drawRoundedRect(r, trackX, cur.y, trackW, rowH, Theme.C.bgInput)
        local frac = math.clamp((newV - minV) / math.max(1e-6, maxV - minV), 0, 1)
        local fillW = math.floor((trackW - 2) * frac)
        if fillW > 2 then
            Theme.drawRoundedRect(math.max(1, r - 1), trackX + 1, cur.y + 1, fillW, rowH - 2,
                cap and Theme.C.accent or (hot and Theme.C.bgSelection or Theme.C.accentSoft), cap and 160 or 190)
        end
        if hot or cap then
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.borderFocus)
            render.drawRectOutline(trackX, cur.y, trackW, rowH, 1)
        else
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.border)
            render.drawRectOutline(trackX, cur.y, trackW, rowH, 1)
        end

        Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Mono)
        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textPrimary)
        render.drawSimpleText(trackX + trackW * 0.5, cur.y + rowH * 0.5, string.format(fmt or "%.2f", newV), TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)

        advanceCursor(cur, totalW, rowH)
        return newV, changed
    end

    function ImGui.SliderInt(label, val, minV, maxV, fmt, w)
        local nv, ch = ImGui.SliderFloat(label, math.floor((val or 0) + 0.5), minV, maxV, fmt or "%.0f", w)
        nv = math.floor(nv + 0.5)
        return nv, (ch or nv ~= val)
    end

    function ImGui.DragFloat(label, val, speed, minV, maxV, fmt, w)
        local cur = currentCursor()
        if not cur then return val, false end
        local ui = ImGui._ui
        local disp = stripIdSuffix(label)
        local totalW = (w and w > 0) and w or getItemWidth(cur)
        local rowH = 22
        local wid = ImGui.GetID("dragf_" .. label)

        local labelW = (disp ~= "") and math.min(math.floor(totalW * 0.42), 130) or 0
        local boxX = cur.x + labelW
        local boxW = math.max(48, totalW - labelW)

        if disp ~= "" then
            Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Body)
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textSecondary)
            render.drawSimpleText(cur.x, cur.y + rowH * 0.5, disp, TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)
        end

        local hot, cap = ui:hitTest(wid, boxX, cur.y, boxW, rowH)
        if hot then
            ui:setTooltip("Drag left/right to scrub value")
            if ui.leftPressed then ui:capture(wid) end
        end

        local out = tonumber(val) or 0
        local changed = false
        if cap and ui.leftDown and ui.dx ~= 0 then
            local sp = speed or 0.1
            out = out + ui.dx * sp
            if minV and maxV then out = math.clamp(out, minV, maxV) end
            changed = (out ~= val)
        end

        Theme.drawRoundedRect(Theme.Style.frameRounding or 4, boxX, cur.y, boxW, rowH,
            cap and Theme.C.bgControlPress or (hot and Theme.C.bgControlHover or Theme.C.bgInput))
        Theme.setColor(cap and Theme.C.borderFocus or (hot and Theme.C.borderLight or Theme.C.border))
        render.drawRectOutline(boxX, cur.y, boxW, rowH, 1)

        Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Mono)
        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textPrimary)
        render.drawSimpleText(boxX + boxW * 0.5, cur.y + rowH * 0.5, string.format(fmt or "%.2f", out), TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)

        advanceCursor(cur, totalW, rowH)
        return out, changed
    end

    local AXIS_COLS = nil

    function ImGui.DragFloat3(label, x, y, z, speed, fmt)
        local cur = currentCursor()
        if not cur then return x, y, z, false end
        local ui = ImGui._ui
        local disp = stripIdSuffix(label)
        local totalW = getItemWidth(cur)
        local rowH = 22
        if disp ~= "" then
            Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textSecondary)
            render.drawSimpleText(cur.x, cur.y, disp, TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.TOP)
            advanceCursor(cur, totalW, 15)
        end

        if not AXIS_COLS then
            AXIS_COLS = { Theme.C.axisX, Theme.C.axisY, Theme.C.axisZ }
        end

        local vals = { tonumber(x) or 0, tonumber(y) or 0, tonumber(z) or 0 }
        local changed = false
        local gap = 4
        local colW = math.max(36, math.floor((totalW - gap * 2) / 3))
        local sp = speed or 0.2
        local fstr = fmt or "%.2f"
        local baseId = ImGui.GetID("df3_" .. label)

        for i = 1, 3 do
            local cx = cur.x + (i - 1) * (colW + gap)
            local wid = baseId .. "_" .. i
            local hot, cap = ui:hitTest(wid, cx, cur.y, colW, rowH)
            if hot then
                ui:setTooltip("Drag left/right to adjust axis")
                if ui.leftPressed then ui:capture(wid) end
            end
            if cap and ui.leftDown and ui.dx ~= 0 then
                vals[i] = vals[i] + ui.dx * sp
                changed = true
            end

            Theme.drawRoundedRect(Theme.Style.frameRounding or 4, cx, cur.y, colW, rowH,
                cap and Theme.C.bgControlPress or (hot and Theme.C.bgControlHover or Theme.C.bgInput))
            Theme.setColor(AXIS_COLS[i])
            render.drawRectFast(cx + 1, cur.y + 2, 3, rowH - 4)
            Theme.setColor(cap and Theme.C.borderFocus or (hot and Theme.C.borderLight or Theme.C.border))
            render.drawRectOutline(cx, cur.y, colW, rowH, 1)

            Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Mono)
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textPrimary)
            render.drawSimpleText(cx + colW * 0.5 + 2, cur.y + rowH * 0.5, string.format(fstr, vals[i]), TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)
        end

        advanceCursor(cur, totalW, rowH)
        return vals[1], vals[2], vals[3], changed
    end

    -- TabBar (Dear ImGui style)
    function ImGui.BeginTabBar(id)
        local cur = currentCursor()
        if not cur then return false end
        local fullId = ImGui.GetID("tabs_" .. id)
        local tb = ImGui._tabBars[fullId]
        if not tb then
            tb = { activeTab = nil }
            ImGui._tabBars[fullId] = tb
        end

        local ctx = {
            id = fullId,
            tb = tb,
            cur = cur,
            x = cur.x,
            y = cur.y,
            tabH = 22,
            startX = cur.x,
            bottomY = cur.y + 23,
            drawnAny = false,
        }
        -- Reserve tab header strip + underline BEFORE tab item contents draw
        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.border)
        render.drawLine(cur.contentX, cur.y + ctx.tabH, cur.contentX + cur.contentW, cur.y + ctx.tabH)
        advanceCursor(cur, cur.contentW, ctx.tabH + 4)

        ImGui._tabStack[#ImGui._tabStack + 1] = ctx
        return true
    end

    function ImGui.BeginTabItem(label, selectedOverride)
        local tn = #ImGui._tabStack
        if tn == 0 then return false end
        local ctx = ImGui._tabStack[tn]
        local ui = ImGui._ui
        local disp = stripIdSuffix(label)
        local tabId = ctx.id .. "/" .. label

        if not ctx.tb.activeTab then
            ctx.tb.activeTab = label
        end
        if selectedOverride == true then
            ctx.tb.activeTab = label
        end

        Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Body)
        local tw = render.getTextSize(disp) or 44
        local tabW = tw + 20
        local tabH = ctx.tabH
        local isSelected = (ctx.tb.activeTab == label)

        local hot, cap = ui:hitTest("tab_" .. tabId, ctx.x, ctx.y, tabW, tabH)
        if hot and ui.leftPressed then ui:capture("tab_" .. tabId) end
        if cap and ui.leftReleased and hot then
            ctx.tb.activeTab = label
            isSelected = true
        end

        local bgCol = isSelected and Theme.C.bgTabActive or (hot and Theme.C.bgTabHover or Theme.C.bgTab)
        Theme.drawRoundedRectEx(Theme.Style.tabRounding or 4, ctx.x, ctx.y, tabW, tabH, true, true, false, false, bgCol)
        if isSelected then
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.accentBright)
            render.drawRectFast(ctx.x + 2, ctx.y + tabH - 2, tabW - 4, 2)
        end

        Theme.setFont(isSelected and Theme.Fonts.BodyBold or Theme.Fonts.Body)
        Theme.setColor(isSelected and Theme.C.textPrimary or Theme.C.textSecondary)
        render.drawSimpleText(ctx.x + tabW * 0.5, ctx.y + tabH * 0.5, disp, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)

        ctx.x = ctx.x + tabW + 3
        ctx.drawnAny = true
        return isSelected
    end

    function ImGui.EndTabItem()
        -- Semantic symmetry with Dear ImGui
    end

    function ImGui.EndTabBar()
        local tn = #ImGui._tabStack
        if tn == 0 then return end
        ImGui._tabStack[tn] = nil
    end

    function ImGui.getTabBarActive(id)
        local fullId = ImGui.GetID("tabs_" .. id)
        local tb = ImGui._tabBars[fullId]
        return tb and tb.activeTab or nil
    end

    function ImGui.setTabBarActive(id, label)
        local fullId = ImGui.GetID("tabs_" .. id)
        ImGui._tabBars[fullId] = ImGui._tabBars[fullId] or {}
        ImGui._tabBars[fullId].activeTab = label
    end

    -- Dropdown / Combo (deferred top-most popup overlay!)
    function ImGui.Combo(label, currentIdx, items, maxShow, w)
        local cur = currentCursor()
        if not cur then return currentIdx, false end
        local ui = ImGui._ui
        local disp = stripIdSuffix(label)
        local totalW = (w and w > 0) and w or getItemWidth(cur)
        local rowH = 22
        local wid = ImGui.GetID("cmb_" .. label)

        local labelW = (disp ~= "") and math.min(math.floor(totalW * 0.42), 130) or 0
        local boxX = cur.x + labelW
        local boxW = math.max(60, totalW - labelW)

        if disp ~= "" then
            Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Body)
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textSecondary)
            render.drawSimpleText(cur.x, cur.y + rowH * 0.5, disp, TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)
        end

        local pending = ImGui._comboPending[wid]
        local outIdx = currentIdx
        local changed = false
        if pending ~= nil then
            ImGui._comboPending[wid] = nil
            if pending ~= currentIdx then
                outIdx = pending
                changed = true
            end
        end

        local isOpen = (ImGui._activePopup and ImGui._activePopup.id == wid)
        local hot, cap = ui:hitTest(wid, boxX, cur.y, boxW, rowH)
        if hot and ui.leftPressed then
            ui:capture(wid)
        end
        if cap and ui.leftReleased and hot then
            if isOpen then
                ImGui._activePopup = nil
                ImGui._activePopupRect = nil
            else
                local visCount = math.min(#items, maxShow or 8)
                local popH = visCount * 22 + 8
                local offX = ui._rtOffsetX or 0
                local offY = ui._rtOffsetY or 0
                local popX = boxX + offX
                local popY = cur.y + offY + rowH + 2
                if popY + popH > (ui.scrH - 8) and (cur.y + offY - popH - 2) >= 8 then
                    popY = cur.y + offY - popH - 2
                end
                ImGui._activePopup = {
                    id = wid,
                    x = popX,
                    y = popY,
                    w = math.max(boxW, 130),
                    h = popH,
                    items = items,
                    selectedIdx = outIdx,
                    scroll = 0,
                    maxShow = maxShow or 8,
                    onPick = function(idx)
                        ImGui._comboPending[wid] = idx
                    end,
                }
                ImGui._activePopupCloseRequested = false
            end
        end

        Theme.drawRoundedRect(Theme.Style.frameRounding or 4, boxX, cur.y, boxW, rowH,
            (isOpen or hot) and Theme.C.bgControlHover or Theme.C.bgControl)
        Theme.setColor((isOpen or hot) and Theme.C.borderFocus or Theme.C.borderLight)
        render.drawRectOutline(boxX, cur.y, boxW, rowH, 1)

        local preview = tostring(items[outIdx] or "")
        Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Body)
        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textPrimary)
        render.drawSimpleText(boxX + 8, cur.y + rowH * 0.5, preview, TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)

        -- Right arrow button strip
        local arrW = 20
        Theme.setColor(isOpen and Theme.C.accent or Theme.C.bgPanelAlt)
        Theme.drawRoundedRectEx(Theme.Style.frameRounding or 4, boxX + boxW - arrW, cur.y + 1, arrW - 1, rowH - 2, false, true, false, true)
        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textPrimary)
        Theme.drawIcon("chevronDown", boxX + boxW - 16, cur.y + 5, 12)

        advanceCursor(cur, totalW, rowH)
        return outIdx, changed
    end

    function ImGui.DrawPopups(ui)
        local p = ImGui._activePopup
        if not p then
            ImGui._activePopupRect = nil
            return
        end

        local items = p.items or {}
        local itemH = 22
        local vis = math.min(#items, p.maxShow or 8)
        local popH = vis * itemH + 8
        local px = math.clamp(p.x, 4, math.max(4, ui.scrW - p.w - 4))
        local py = math.clamp(p.y, 4, math.max(4, ui.scrH - popH - 4))
        p.x, p.y, p.h = px, py, popH
        ImGui._activePopupRect = { px, py, p.w, popH }

        ui:setWindowContext("__popup__", true)

        if #items > vis and ui.wheelDelta ~= 0 and ui:pointInRect(px, py, p.w, popH) then
            p.scroll = math.clamp((p.scroll or 0) - math.floor(ui.wheelDelta), 0, #items - vis)
        end

        -- Drop shadow + popup surface
        Theme.drawRoundedRect(Theme.Style.popupRounding or 4, px + 2, py + 3, p.w, popH, Theme.C.bgApp, 150)
        Theme.drawRoundedRect(Theme.Style.popupRounding or 4, px, py, p.w, popH, Theme.C.bgPopup)
        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.borderFocus)
        render.drawRectOutline(px, py, p.w, popH, 1)

        local startIdx = (p.scroll or 0) + 1
        local endIdx = math.min(#items, startIdx + vis - 1)
        for i = startIdx, endIdx do
            local rowIdx = i - startIdx
            local iy = py + 4 + rowIdx * itemH
            local isSel = (i == p.selectedIdx)
            local hot, cap = ui:hitTest(p.id .. "_opt_" .. i, px + 4, iy, p.w - 8, itemH)
            if hot and ui.leftPressed then
                ui:capture(p.id .. "_opt_" .. i)
            end
            if cap and ui.leftReleased and hot then
                if p.onPick then p.onPick(i) end
                ImGui._activePopup = nil
                ImGui._activePopupRect = nil
                ImGui._activePopupCloseRequested = false
                ui:setWindowContext(nil, false)
                return
            end

            if isSel then
                Theme.drawRoundedRect(3, px + 4, iy, p.w - 8, itemH, Theme.C.bgSelectionRow)
            elseif hot then
                Theme.drawRoundedRect(3, px + 4, iy, p.w - 8, itemH, Theme.C.bgControlHover)
            end

            Theme.setFont(isSel and Theme.Fonts.BodyBold or Theme.Fonts.Body)
            Theme.setColor(isSel and Theme.C.textPrimary or Theme.C.textSecondary)
            render.drawSimpleText(px + 10, iy + itemH * 0.5, tostring(items[i]), TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)
        end

        ui:setWindowContext(nil, false)
    end

    function ImGui.InputText(label, value, placeholder, w)
        local cur = currentCursor()
        if not cur then return value, false, false end
        local ui = ImGui._ui
        local disp = stripIdSuffix(label)
        local totalW = (w and w > 0) and w or getItemWidth(cur)
        local rowH = 22
        local wid = ImGui.GetID("inp_" .. label)

        ImGui._inputValues = ImGui._inputValues or {}
        ImGui._inputPending = ImGui._inputPending or {}
        local pending = ImGui._inputPending[wid]
        local curText = (pending ~= nil) and pending or tostring(value or "")
        ImGui._inputPending[wid] = nil
        ImGui._inputValues[wid] = curText

        local labelW = (disp ~= "") and math.min(math.floor(totalW * 0.42), 130) or 0
        local boxX = cur.x + labelW
        local boxW = math.max(60, totalW - labelW)

        if disp ~= "" then
            Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Body)
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textSecondary)
            render.drawSimpleText(cur.x, cur.y + rowH * 0.5, disp, TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)
        end

        local clearedNow = false
        Widgets.textBox(
            ui,
            wid,
            boxX,
            cur.y,
            boxW,
            rowH,
            function() return ImGui._inputValues[wid] end,
            function(v)
                ImGui._inputValues[wid] = v
                ImGui._inputPending[wid] = v
                clearedNow = true
            end,
            placeholder
        )

        local newVal = ImGui._inputValues[wid]
        if clearedNow then
            ImGui._inputPending[wid] = nil
        end
        local changed = (pending ~= nil) or clearedNow
        advanceCursor(cur, totalW, rowH)
        return newVal, changed, false
    end

    function ImGui.CollapsingHeader(label, defaultOpen)
        local cur = currentCursor()
        if not cur then return false end
        local ui = ImGui._ui
        local disp = stripIdSuffix(label)
        local wid = ImGui.GetID("colhdr_" .. label)
        if ImGui._collapsing[wid] == nil then
            ImGui._collapsing[wid] = (defaultOpen ~= false)
        end
        local isOpen = ImGui._collapsing[wid]
        local w = cur.contentW
        local h = 22

        local hot, cap = ui:hitTest(wid, cur.x, cur.y, w, h)
        if hot and ui.leftPressed then ui:capture(wid) end
        if cap and ui.leftReleased and hot then
            isOpen = not isOpen
            ImGui._collapsing[wid] = isOpen
        end

        Theme.drawRoundedRect(3, cur.x, cur.y, w, h, hot and Theme.C.bgControlHover or Theme.C.bgHeader)
        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.border)
        render.drawRectOutline(cur.x, cur.y, w, h, 1)
        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textSecondary)
        Theme.drawIcon(isOpen and "chevronDown" or "chevronRight", cur.x + 5, cur.y + 5, 12)
        Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.BodyBold)
        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textPrimary)
        render.drawSimpleText(cur.x + 22, cur.y + h * 0.5, disp, TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)

        advanceCursor(cur, w, h)
        return isOpen
    end

    function ImGui.TreeNode(label, defaultOpen)
        local cur = currentCursor()
        if not cur then return false end
        local ui = ImGui._ui
        local disp = stripIdSuffix(label)
        local wid = ImGui.GetID("tree_" .. label)
        if ImGui._collapsing[wid] == nil then
            ImGui._collapsing[wid] = (defaultOpen == true)
        end
        local isOpen = ImGui._collapsing[wid]
        local w = math.max(40, cur.contentW - (cur.x - cur.contentX))
        local h = 20

        local hot, cap = ui:hitTest(wid, cur.x, cur.y, w, h)
        if hot and ui.leftPressed then ui:capture(wid) end
        if cap and ui.leftReleased and hot then
            isOpen = not isOpen
            ImGui._collapsing[wid] = isOpen
        end

        if hot then
            Theme.drawRoundedRect(3, cur.x, cur.y, w, h, Theme.C.bgControlHover, 140)
        end
        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textSecondary)
        Theme.drawIcon(isOpen and "chevronDown" or "chevronRight", cur.x + 3, cur.y + 4, 12)
        Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Body)
        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textPrimary)
        render.drawSimpleText(cur.x + 20, cur.y + h * 0.5, disp, TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)

        advanceCursor(cur, w, h)
        if isOpen then
            ImGui.Indent(14)
            ImGui.PushID(label)
            ImGui._treeStack[#ImGui._treeStack + 1] = true
        end
        return isOpen
    end

    function ImGui.TreePop()
        local n = #ImGui._treeStack
        if n == 0 then return end
        ImGui._treeStack[n] = nil
        ImGui.PopID()
        ImGui.Unindent(14)
    end

    function ImGui.ProgressBar(fraction, w, h, overlayText)
        local cur = currentCursor()
        if not cur then return end
        local frac = math.clamp(fraction or 0, 0, 1)
        local pw = (w and w > 0) and w or getItemWidth(cur)
        local ph = (h and h > 0) and h or 18
        local r = math.min(4, Theme.Style.frameRounding or 4)

        Theme.drawRoundedRect(r, cur.x, cur.y, pw, ph, Theme.C.bgInput)
        local fillW = math.floor(pw * frac)
        if fillW > 2 then
            Theme.drawRoundedRect(r, cur.x + 1, cur.y + 1, fillW - 2, ph - 2, Theme.C.accent)
        end
        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.border)
        render.drawRectOutline(cur.x, cur.y, pw, ph, 1)

        local label = overlayText or string.format("%d%%", math.floor(frac * 100 + 0.5))
        Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textPrimary)
        render.drawSimpleText(cur.x + pw * 0.5, cur.y + ph * 0.5, label, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)

        advanceCursor(cur, pw, ph)
    end

    -- Toast Notification Queue (ImGui.Notify + ImGui.DrawNotifications)
    function ImGui.Notify(title, message, kind, duration)
        kind = kind or "info"
        duration = duration or 3.5
        local list = ImGui._notifications
        -- Avoid spamming identical consecutive toasts
        local top = list[#list]
        if top and top.title == title and top.message == message and top.age < 0.8 then
            top.age = 0
            return
        end
        list[#list + 1] = {
            id = "ntf_" .. tostring(ImGui._frameCount) .. "_" .. tostring(#list + 1),
            title = tostring(title or "Notice"),
            message = tostring(message or ""),
            kind = kind,
            age = 0,
            duration = duration,
        }
        while #list > 5 do
            table.remove(list, 1)
        end
    end

    function ImGui.DrawNotifications(ui, layout)
        local list = ImGui._notifications
        if #list == 0 then return end

        local cardW = 290
        local cardH = 48
        local gap = 8
        local rightEdge = (layout and layout.vp and (layout.vp.x + layout.vp.w - 12)) or (ui.scrW - 14)
        local baseY = (layout and layout.vp and (layout.vp.y + layout.vp.h - 14)) or (ui.scrH - 18)

        for idx = #list, 1, -1 do
            local n = list[idx]
            local rev = #list - idx
            local fade = 1
            if n.age < 0.18 then
                fade = n.age / 0.18
            elseif (n.duration - n.age) < 0.35 then
                fade = math.max(0, (n.duration - n.age) / 0.35)
            end
            local alpha = math.floor(240 * fade)
            local x = rightEdge - cardW + math.floor((1 - fade) * 18)
            local y = baseY - (rev + 1) * cardH - rev * gap

            local col = Theme.C.accent
            local icon = "info"
            if n.kind == "success" then
                col = Theme.C.success
                icon = "check"
            elseif n.kind == "warning" then
                col = Theme.C.warning
                icon = "warning"
            elseif n.kind == "error" then
                col = Theme.C.danger
                icon = "warning"
            end

            Theme.drawRoundedRect(5, x, y, cardW, cardH, Theme.C.bgPopup, alpha)
            Theme.setColor(col, alpha)
            render.drawRectFast(x, y + 3, 3, cardH - 6)
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.borderLight, alpha)
            render.drawRectOutline(x, y, cardW, cardH, 1)

            Theme.setColor(col, alpha)
            Theme.drawIcon(icon, x + 12, y + 10, 14)

            Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.BodyBold)
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textPrimary, alpha)
            render.drawSimpleText(x + 32, y + 9, n.title, TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.TOP)

            if n.message ~= "" then
                Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
                Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textSecondary, alpha)
                render.drawSimpleText(x + 32, y + 26, n.message, TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.TOP)
            end

            local clsHot, _ = ui:hitTest(n.id .. "_cls", x + cardW - 22, y + 6, 16, 16)
            if clsHot and ui.leftPressed then
                table.remove(list, idx)
            else
                Theme.setColor(clsHot and Theme.C.textPrimary or Theme.C.textMuted, alpha)
                Theme.drawIcon("hide", x + cardW - 18, y + 8, 10)
            end
        end
    end

    SOLACE.ImGui = ImGui

    -- 13.5 Resizable Dock Layout & Splitters


    local Layout = {}
    Layout.__index = Layout

    function Layout.new(state)
        local d = Constants.LAYOUT_DEFAULTS
        return setmetatable({
            state = state,
            toolbarH = d.toolbarHeight,
            leftW = d.leftWidth,
            rightW = d.rightWidth,
            bottomH = d.bottomHeight,
            explorerRatio = d.explorerRatio,
            _customized = false,
            _lastScrW = 0,
            _lastScrH = 0,
            rects = {
                toolbar = { x = 0, y = 0, w = 0, h = 0 },
                toolbox = { x = 0, y = 0, w = 0, h = 0 },
                viewport = { x = 0, y = 0, w = 0, h = 0 },
                explorer = { x = 0, y = 0, w = 0, h = 0 },
                properties = { x = 0, y = 0, w = 0, h = 0 },
                timeline = { x = 0, y = 0, w = 0, h = 0 },
            },
            _splitters = {},
        }, Layout)
    end

    function Layout:applyResponsiveDefaults(scrW, scrH)
        local d = Constants.LAYOUT_DEFAULTS
        self.toolbarH = (scrH < 820) and 34 or d.toolbarHeight
        self.leftW = math.clamp(math.floor(scrW * 0.145), d.minLeftWidth, d.maxLeftWidth)
        if self.leftW > 244 then self.leftW = 244 end
        self.rightW = math.clamp(math.floor(scrW * 0.175), d.minRightWidth, d.maxRightWidth)
        if self.rightW > 288 then self.rightW = 288 end
        self.bottomH = math.clamp(math.floor(scrH * 0.18), d.minBottomHeight, d.maxBottomHeight)
        if self.bottomH > 184 then self.bottomH = 184 end
        self.explorerRatio = d.explorerRatio
    end

    function Layout:reset()
        self._customized = false
        if self._lastScrW > 0 and self._lastScrH > 0 then
            self:applyResponsiveDefaults(self._lastScrW, self._lastScrH)
        else
            local d = Constants.LAYOUT_DEFAULTS
            self.toolbarH = d.toolbarHeight
            self.leftW = d.leftWidth
            self.rightW = d.rightWidth
            self.bottomH = d.bottomHeight
            self.explorerRatio = d.explorerRatio
        end
    end

    function Layout:compute(scrW, scrH)
        local d = Constants.LAYOUT_DEFAULTS
        if scrW ~= self._lastScrW or scrH ~= self._lastScrH then
            self._lastScrW = scrW
            self._lastScrH = scrH
            if not self._customized then
                self:applyResponsiveDefaults(scrW, scrH)
            end
        end
        local sp = d.splitterSize
        local panels = self.state.panels
        local fp = self.state.floatingPanels or {}

        local showToolbox = panels.toolbox and not fp.toolbox
        local showExplorer = panels.explorer and not fp.explorer
        local showProperties = panels.properties and not fp.properties
        local showTimeline = panels.timeline and not fp.timeline

        local showLeft = showToolbox
        local showRight = showExplorer or showProperties
        local showBottom = showTimeline

        local topY = self.toolbarH
        local availH = math.max(200, scrH - topY)

        -- Clamp bottom timeline height
        local activeBottomH = 0
        if showBottom then
            local maxB = math.min(d.maxBottomHeight, availH - d.minViewportHeight - sp)
            self.bottomH = math.clamp(self.bottomH, d.minBottomHeight, math.max(d.minBottomHeight, maxB))
            activeBottomH = self.bottomH
        end

        local middleH = availH - (showBottom and (activeBottomH + sp) or 0)

        -- Clamp left & right widths while preserving minimum center viewport width
        local activeLeftW = showLeft and math.clamp(self.leftW, d.minLeftWidth, d.maxLeftWidth) or 0
        local activeRightW = showRight and math.clamp(self.rightW, d.minRightWidth, d.maxRightWidth) or 0

        local neededNonViewport = (showLeft and (activeLeftW + sp) or 0) + (showRight and (activeRightW + sp) or 0)
        if scrW - neededNonViewport < d.minViewportWidth then
            local maxCombined = math.max(0, scrW - d.minViewportWidth - (showLeft and sp or 0) - (showRight and sp or 0))
            if showLeft and showRight then
                local ratio = activeLeftW / math.max(1, activeLeftW + activeRightW)
                activeLeftW = math.floor(maxCombined * ratio)
                activeRightW = maxCombined - activeLeftW
            elseif showLeft then
                activeLeftW = maxCombined
            elseif showRight then
                activeRightW = maxCombined
            end
        end

        self.leftW = showLeft and activeLeftW or self.leftW
        self.rightW = showRight and activeRightW or self.rightW

        local r = self.rects
        r.toolbar = { x = 0, y = 0, w = scrW, h = self.toolbarH }

        local curX = 0
        if showLeft then
            r.toolbox = { x = 0, y = topY, w = activeLeftW, h = middleH }
            curX = activeLeftW + sp
        else
            r.toolbox = { x = 0, y = 0, w = 0, h = 0 }
        end

        local vpW = scrW - curX - (showRight and (activeRightW + sp) or 0)
        r.viewport = { x = curX, y = topY, w = math.max(0, vpW), h = middleH }

        local rightX = curX + r.viewport.w + (showRight and sp or 0)
        if showExplorer and showProperties then
            local expH = math.floor((middleH - sp) * math.clamp(self.explorerRatio, d.minExplorerRatio, d.maxExplorerRatio))
            local propY = topY + expH + sp
            r.explorer = { x = rightX, y = topY, w = activeRightW, h = expH }
            r.properties = { x = rightX, y = propY, w = activeRightW, h = math.max(0, middleH - expH - sp) }
        elseif showExplorer then
            r.explorer = { x = rightX, y = topY, w = activeRightW, h = middleH }
            r.properties = { x = 0, y = 0, w = 0, h = 0 }
        elseif showProperties then
            r.explorer = { x = 0, y = 0, w = 0, h = 0 }
            r.properties = { x = rightX, y = topY, w = activeRightW, h = middleH }
        else
            r.explorer = { x = 0, y = 0, w = 0, h = 0 }
            r.properties = { x = 0, y = 0, w = 0, h = 0 }
        end

        if showBottom then
            r.timeline = { x = 0, y = topY + middleH + sp, w = scrW, h = activeBottomH }
        else
            r.timeline = { x = 0, y = 0, w = 0, h = 0 }
        end

        -- Build active splitter hitboxes
        self._splitters = {}
        if showLeft then
            self._splitters[#self._splitters + 1] = {
                id = "split_left",
                axis = "x",
                x = r.toolbox.w,
                y = topY,
                w = sp,
                h = middleH,
            }
        end
        if showRight then
            self._splitters[#self._splitters + 1] = {
                id = "split_right",
                axis = "x",
                x = r.viewport.x + r.viewport.w,
                y = topY,
                w = sp,
                h = middleH,
            }
        end
        if showExplorer and showProperties then
            self._splitters[#self._splitters + 1] = {
                id = "split_right_y",
                axis = "y",
                x = rightX,
                y = r.explorer.y + r.explorer.h,
                w = activeRightW,
                h = sp,
                parentTop = topY,
                parentH = middleH - sp,
            }
        end
        if showBottom then
            self._splitters[#self._splitters + 1] = {
                id = "split_bottom",
                axis = "y",
                x = 0,
                y = topY + middleH,
                w = scrW,
                h = sp,
            }
        end
    end

    function Layout:updateAndDrawSplitters(ui)
        local d = Constants.LAYOUT_DEFAULTS
        for _, s in ipairs(self._splitters) do
            local hot, captured = ui:hitTest(s.id, s.x - 1, s.y - 1, s.w + 2, s.h + 2)
            if hot then
                ui:setTooltip("Drag to resize panel")
            end
            if hot and ui.leftPressed then
                ui:capture(s.id)
            end

            if captured and ui.leftDown then
                self._customized = true
                if s.id == "split_left" then
                    self.leftW = math.clamp(ui.mx, d.minLeftWidth, d.maxLeftWidth)
                elseif s.id == "split_right" then
                    self.rightW = math.clamp(ui.scrW - ui.mx, d.minRightWidth, d.maxRightWidth)
                elseif s.id == "split_bottom" then
                    self.bottomH = math.clamp(ui.scrH - ui.my, d.minBottomHeight, d.maxBottomHeight)
                elseif s.id == "split_right_y" then
                    local frac = (ui.my - s.parentTop) / math.max(1, s.parentH)
                    self.explorerRatio = math.clamp(frac, d.minExplorerRatio, d.maxExplorerRatio)
                end
            end

            Theme.setColor((hot or captured) and Theme.C.splitterHover or Theme.C.splitter)
            render.drawRectFast(s.x, s.y, s.w, s.h)

            -- Subtle grip indicator
            Theme.setColor((hot or captured) and Theme.C.textPrimary or Theme.C.borderLight)
            if s.axis == "x" then
                local cy = s.y + s.h * 0.5
                render.drawRectFast(s.x + 1, cy - 12, s.w - 2, 24)
            else
                local cx = s.x + s.w * 0.5
                render.drawRectFast(cx - 14, s.y + 1, 28, s.h - 2)
            end
        end
    end

    SOLACE.Layout = Layout
end)(SOLACE) end

-- ============================================================================
-- SECTION 14: UI panels
-- ============================================================================
do (function(SOLACE)
    local Constants = SOLACE.Constants
    local Utils = SOLACE.Utils
    local Theme = SOLACE.Theme
    local Widgets = SOLACE.Widgets

    -- 14.1 3D Viewport Grid & Overlays


    local ViewportGrid = {}

    -- Reusable scratch vectors for zero-allocation 3D line drawing
    local pA = Vector(0, 0, 0)
    local pB = Vector(0, 0, 0)

    local AXIS_VECS = {
        { label = "X", vec = Vector(1, 0, 0), color = Theme.C.axisX },
        { label = "Y", vec = Vector(0, 1, 0), color = Theme.C.axisY },
        { label = "Z", vec = Vector(0, 0, 1), color = Theme.C.axisZ },
    }

    local function setVec(v, x, y, z)
        v[1] = x
        v[2] = y
        v[3] = z
    end

    function ViewportGrid.resolveOrigin(state)
        local g = state.grid
        if g.originMode == "chip" and isValid(chip()) then
            local p = chip():getPos()
            g.originPos[1], g.originPos[2], g.originPos[3] = p[1], p[2], p[3]
        elseif g.originMode == "player" and isValid(player()) then
            local p = player():getPos()
            g.originPos[1], g.originPos[2], g.originPos[3] = p[1], p[2], p[3]
        else
            g.originPos[1], g.originPos[2], g.originPos[3] = 0, 0, 0
        end
    end

    function ViewportGrid.draw3D(state)
        if not state.studioOpen then return end
        local g = state.grid
        if not g.enabled and not g.showAxes then return end

        local ox = g.originPos[1]
        local oy = g.originPos[2]
        local oz = g.originPos[3] + (g.zOffset or 0.25)

        local spacing = g.spacing
        local n = g.halfExtents
        local majorEvery = math.max(1, g.majorEvery)
        local span = n * spacing

        if g.enabled then
            local skipZero = g.showAxes
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.gridMinor)
            for i = -n, n do
                if not (i == 0 and skipZero) and (i % majorEvery ~= 0) then
                    local offset = i * spacing
                    setVec(pA, ox - span, oy + offset, oz)
                    setVec(pB, ox + span, oy + offset, oz)
                    render.draw3DLine(pA, pB, true)

                    setVec(pA, ox + offset, oy - span, oz)
                    setVec(pB, ox + offset, oy + span, oz)
                    render.draw3DLine(pA, pB, true)
                end
            end

            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.gridMajor)
            for i = -n, n do
                if not (i == 0 and skipZero) and (i % majorEvery == 0) then
                    local offset = i * spacing
                    setVec(pA, ox - span, oy + offset, oz)
                    setVec(pB, ox + span, oy + offset, oz)
                    render.draw3DLine(pA, pB, true)

                    setVec(pA, ox + offset, oy - span, oz)
                    setVec(pB, ox + offset, oy + span, oz)
                    render.draw3DLine(pA, pB, true)
                end
            end
        end

        if g.showAxes then
            local axisLen = math.max(spacing * 2, span)
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.axisX, 230)
            setVec(pA, ox, oy, oz)
            setVec(pB, ox + axisLen, oy, oz)
            render.draw3DLine(pA, pB, true)
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.axisX, 75)
            setVec(pB, ox - span, oy, oz)
            render.draw3DLine(pA, pB, true)

            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.axisY, 230)
            setVec(pA, ox, oy, oz)
            setVec(pB, ox, oy + axisLen, oz)
            render.draw3DLine(pA, pB, true)
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.axisY, 75)
            setVec(pB, ox, oy - span, oz)
            render.draw3DLine(pA, pB, true)

            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.axisZ, 235)
            setVec(pA, ox, oy, oz)
            setVec(pB, ox, oy, oz + math.min(axisLen, spacing * 4))
            render.draw3DLine(pA, pB, true)
        end

        if g.showOriginMarker then
            local r = math.max(4, spacing * 0.14)
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.warning, 220)
            setVec(pA, ox - r, oy - r, oz)
            setVec(pB, ox + r, oy + r, oz)
            render.draw3DLine(pA, pB, true)
            setVec(pA, ox - r, oy + r, oz)
            setVec(pB, ox + r, oy - r, oz)
            render.draw3DLine(pA, pB, true)
        end
    end

    local TRIAD_ITEMS = {
        { label = "X", color = Theme.C.axisX, sx = 0, sy = 0, depth = 0 },
        { label = "Y", color = Theme.C.axisY, sx = 0, sy = 0, depth = 0 },
        { label = "Z", color = Theme.C.axisZ, sx = 0, sy = 0, depth = 0 },
    }
    local function sortTriad(a, b) return a.depth < b.depth end

    local function drawOrientationTriad(cx, cy, radius, ang)
        local bgR = radius + 12
        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.hudPill)
        render.drawRectFast(cx - bgR, cy - bgR, bgR * 2, bgR * 2)
        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.borderLight)
        render.drawRectOutline(cx - bgR, cy - bgR, bgR * 2, bgR * 2, 1)

        local fwd = ang:getForward()
        local right = ang:getRight()
        local up = ang:getUp()

        for i = 1, 3 do
            local a = AXIS_VECS[i]
            local it = TRIAD_ITEMS[i]
            it.label = a.label
            it.color = a.color
            it.sx = a.vec:dot(right)
            it.sy = -a.vec:dot(up)
            it.depth = a.vec:dot(fwd)
        end
        table.sort(TRIAD_ITEMS, sortTriad)

        Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
        for i = 1, 3 do
            local item = TRIAD_ITEMS[i]
            local ex = cx + item.sx * radius
            local ey = cy + item.sy * radius
            local alpha = item.depth < -0.15 and 120 or 255
            Theme.setColor(item.color, alpha)
            render.drawLine(cx, cy, ex, ey)
            render.drawRectFast(ex - 6, ey - 6, 12, 12)
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textPrimary, alpha)
            render.drawSimpleText(ex, ey, item.label, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)
        end
    end

    function ViewportGrid.updateLookMode(state, ui, vpRect)
        if not state.studioOpen then
            state.viewportLooking = false
            return
        end

        local overFloating = (ui._topHoverWinId ~= nil) or (SOLACE.ImGui and SOLACE.ImGui.isPointOverAnyWindow and SOLACE.ImGui.isPointOverAnyWindow(ui.mx, ui.my))
        local inViewport = ui:pointInRect(vpRect.x, vpRect.y, vpRect.w, vpRect.h) and not overFloating
        if ui.rightPressed and inViewport and not ui.activeId and not ui.focusedInputId then
            state.viewportLooking = true
            if hasPermission("input.enablecursor") then
                input.enableCursor(false)
            end
        elseif state.viewportLooking and not ui.rightDown then
            state.viewportLooking = false
            if hasPermission("input.enablecursor") then
                input.enableCursor(true)
            end
        end
    end

    local MODE_HINT_IDLE = "Freecam: Hold RMB + WASD/Space/Ctrl  |  Click: Select  |  F: Focus  |  Del: Delete"
    local _cachedModeKey = nil
    local _cachedModeStr = MODE_HINT_IDLE
    local _cachedModeW = nil
    local _lastStatsTime = -1
    local _cachedStatsStr = ""
    local _cachedStatsW = 0
    local _cachedStatsCpuWarn = false

    function ViewportGrid.drawOverlay(state, ui, vpRect, fpsSmooth, camera, sceneGraph)
        local x, y, w, h = vpRect.x, vpRect.y, vpRect.w, vpRect.h
        if w < 40 or h < 40 then return end

        ui:pushClip(x, y, w, h)

        Theme.setColor(state.viewportLooking and Theme.C.accent or Theme.C.border)
        render.drawRectOutline(x, y, w, h, 1)

        -- Top-left Freecam / Selection hint pill
        local pillX = x + 10
        local pillY = y + 10
        local modeKey = state.viewportLooking and (state.camera.speed .. ":" .. state.camera.boostMult) or "idle"
        Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
        if modeKey ~= _cachedModeKey or not _cachedModeW then
            _cachedModeKey = modeKey
            _cachedModeStr = state.viewportLooking
                and string.format("FREECAM  |  Speed: %d u/s (Shift: x%.1f)  |  Release RMB for cursor", state.camera.speed, state.camera.boostMult)
                or MODE_HINT_IDLE
            _cachedModeW = render.getTextSize(_cachedModeStr)
        end
        local pw = math.min(w - 110, _cachedModeW + 18)
        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.hudPill)
        render.drawRectFast(pillX, pillY, pw, 22)
        Theme.setColor(state.viewportLooking and Theme.C.accent or Theme.C.border)
        render.drawRectOutline(pillX, pillY, pw, 22, 1)
        Theme.setColor(state.viewportLooking and Theme.C.accentBright or Theme.C.textSecondary)
        render.drawSimpleText(pillX + 9, pillY + 11, _cachedModeStr, TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)

        -- Top-right 3D Orientation Triad synced with Freecam angle
        if state.grid.showOrientationGizmo and w > 180 and h > 140 then
            local triadAng = (camera and camera.initialized) and camera.camAng or eyeAngles()
            drawOrientationTriad(x + w - 42, y + 42, 22, triadAng)
        end

        -- Bottom-left live telemetry & selection readout (throttled to 5 Hz)
        if state.grid.showTelemetry then
            Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Mono)
            local now = ui._frameTime
            if now - _lastStatsTime >= 0.2 or _cachedStatsStr == "" then
                _lastStatsTime = now
                local cpuAvgFn = cpuAverage or quotaAverage
                local cpuMaxFn = cpuMax or quotaMax
                local cpuMs = cpuAvgFn and (cpuAvgFn() * 1000) or 0
                local maxMs = cpuMaxFn and (cpuMaxFn() * 1000) or 15
                local ramMb = ramUsed and (ramUsed() / 1024) or 0
                local holos = (hologram and hologram.hologramsLeft) and hologram.hologramsLeft() or 0
                local selCount = #sceneGraph:getSelectedIds()
                local selNode = sceneGraph:getSelectedNode()
                local selText = selCount == 0 and "None"
                    or (selCount == 1 and (selNode and selNode.name or "?") or (selCount .. " objects"))

                _cachedStatsStr = string.format(
                    "FPS: %d  |  CPU: %.2f/%.1fms  |  RAM: %.1fMB  |  Holos: %d  |  Cam: %d u/s FOV %d  |  Sel: %s",
                    math.round(fpsSmooth),
                    cpuMs,
                    maxMs,
                    ramMb,
                    holos,
                    state.camera.speed,
                    state.camera.fov,
                    selText
                )
                _cachedStatsW = render.getTextSize(_cachedStatsStr)
                _cachedStatsCpuWarn = (cpuMs / math.max(0.001, maxMs)) > 0.7
            end

            local bx = x + 10
            local by = y + h - 30
            local bw = math.min(w - 20, _cachedStatsW + 16)
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.hudPill)
            render.drawRectFast(bx, by, bw, 20)
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.border)
            render.drawRectOutline(bx, by, bw, 20, 1)

            Theme.setColor(_cachedStatsCpuWarn and Theme.C.warning or Theme.C.textSecondary)
            render.drawSimpleText(bx + 8, by + 10, _cachedStatsStr, TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)
        end

        ui:popClip()
    end

    SOLACE.ViewportGrid = ViewportGrid

    -- 14.2 Top Studio Toolbar


    local Toolbar = {}
    local _brandW = nil
    local TOOLBAR_UPCOMING = {
        { id = "save",   icon = "save",   w = 56, label = "Save",   tip = "Save Project — Arriving in Phase 12" },
        { id = "export", icon = "export", w = 68, label = "Export", tip = "Export StarfallEx Lua — Arriving in Phase 13" },
    }
    local TOOLBAR_PANELS = {
        { key = "timeline",   label = "TL",   tip = "Toggle Timeline Panel" },
        { key = "properties", label = "Prop", tip = "Toggle Properties Panel" },
        { key = "explorer",   label = "Exp",  tip = "Toggle Explorer Panel" },
        { key = "toolbox",    label = "Box",  tip = "Toggle Toolbox Panel" },
    }
    local TOOLBAR_WINDOWS = {
        { key = "console",  label = "Log",   icon = "console", tip = "Toggle Console & Event Log Window" },
        { key = "settings", label = "Prefs", icon = "sliders", tip = "Toggle Studio & ImGui Theme/Style Settings Window" },
        { key = "demo",     label = "ImGui", icon = "sparkle", tip = "Toggle Dear ImGui Widget Showcase (Use Prefs to change Theme/Style)" },
    }

    function Toolbar.draw(ui, state, layout, rect, actions)
        local x, y, w, h = rect.x, rect.y, rect.w, rect.h
        if w < 100 or h <= 0 then return end

        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.bgToolbar)
        render.drawRectFast(x, y, w, h)
        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.border)
        render.drawLine(x, y + h - 1, x + w, y + h - 1)

        ui:pushClip(x, y, w, h)

        local btnH = math.min(26, h - 8)
        local by = y + math.floor((h - btnH) * 0.5)
        local compactBar = (w < 1440)

        -- Right-aligned working panel toggles, ImGui windows, Reset Layout & Hide Studio (F6)
        local rx = x + w - 8

        local hideW = compactBar and 68 or 78
        rx = rx - hideW
        if Widgets.button(ui, "tb_hide", rx, by, hideW, btnH, "Hide F6", {
            icon = "hide",
            tooltip = "Minimize SOLACE Studio and restore gameplay camera/cursor (Press F6 to reopen)",
        }) then
            actions.toggleStudio(false)
        end
        rx = rx - 5

        local resetW = compactBar and 48 or 54
        rx = rx - resetW
        if Widgets.button(ui, "tb_reset_layout", rx, by, resetW, btnH, "Reset", {
            icon = "reset",
            tooltip = "Reset all dock panel sizes and re-dock floating panels",
        }) then
            layout:reset()
            if state.floatingPanels then
                state.floatingPanels.toolbox = false
                state.floatingPanels.explorer = false
                state.floatingPanels.properties = false
                state.floatingPanels.timeline = false
            end
            if SOLACE.ImGui and SOLACE.ImGui.Notify then
                SOLACE.ImGui.Notify("Layout Reset", "Restored default docked studio layout", "info", 2.5)
            end
        end
        rx = rx - 6

        local panelW = compactBar and 34 or 40
        for _, p in ipairs(TOOLBAR_PANELS) do
            rx = rx - panelW
            if rx > x + 90 then
                if Widgets.button(ui, "tb_panel_" .. p.key, rx, by, panelW, btnH, p.label, {
                    active = state.panels[p.key],
                    tooltip = p.tip,
                }) then
                    state.panels[p.key] = not state.panels[p.key]
                end
            end
            rx = rx - 3
        end

        rx = rx - 5
        local winBtnW = compactBar and 46 or 58
        if state.windows then
            for _, witem in ipairs(TOOLBAR_WINDOWS) do
                rx = rx - winBtnW
                if rx > x + 90 then
                    if Widgets.button(ui, "tb_win_" .. witem.key, rx, by, winBtnW, btnH, witem.label, {
                        icon = compactBar and nil or witem.icon,
                        active = state.windows[witem.key],
                        tooltip = witem.tip,
                    }) then
                        local nextOpen = not state.windows[witem.key]
                        state.windows[witem.key] = nextOpen
                        if nextOpen and SOLACE.ImGui then
                            SOLACE.ImGui.bringToFront("win_" .. witem.key)
                        end
                    end
                end
                rx = rx - 3
            end
        end

        local maxLeftX = rx - 8
        local cx = x + 10

        -- Brand identity badge
        Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Title)
        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.accentBright)
        render.drawSimpleText(cx, y + h * 0.5, Constants.APP_NAME, TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)
        if not _brandW then
            _brandW = render.getTextSize(Constants.APP_NAME)
        end
        cx = cx + _brandW + 8

        if not compactBar then
            Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textMuted)
            render.drawSimpleText(cx, y + h * 0.5, "P2", TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)
            cx = cx + 22
        end

        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.border)
        render.drawLine(cx, y + 7, cx, y + h - 7)
        cx = cx + 8

        -- Tool buttons (icon-only for upcoming tools on narrow screens so all actions fit cleanly)
        for _, tool in ipairs(Constants.TOOLS) do
            local useLabel = (not compactBar) or tool.ready
            local tw = useLabel and (compactBar and 64 or 72) or 28
            if cx + tw > maxLeftX then break end
            local clicked = Widgets.button(ui, "tb_tool_" .. tool.id, cx, by, tw, btnH, useLabel and tool.label or nil, {
                icon = tool.icon,
                active = tool.ready and (state.activeTool == tool.id),
                disabled = not tool.ready,
                tooltip = tool.desc,
            })
            if clicked and tool.ready then
                if actions.setTool then
                    actions.setTool(tool.id)
                else
                    state.activeTool = tool.id
                end
            end
            cx = cx + tw + 4
        end

        if cx + 10 <= maxLeftX then
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.border)
            render.drawLine(cx + 2, y + 7, cx + 2, y + h - 7)
            cx = cx + 10
        end

        -- Phase 2 Quick Actions: Focus Selected & Quick Spawn Cube
        local focusW = compactBar and 58 or 68
        if cx + focusW <= maxLeftX then
            if Widgets.button(ui, "tb_focus", cx, by, focusW, btnH, "Focus", {
                icon = "camera",
                disabled = not actions.hasSelection,
                tooltip = "Focus Freecam on Selected Object (Hotkey: F)",
            }) then
                actions.focusSelected()
            end
            cx = cx + focusW + 4
        end

        local cubeW = compactBar and 62 or 72
        if cx + cubeW <= maxLeftX then
            if Widgets.button(ui, "tb_spawn_cube", cx, by, cubeW, btnH, "+Cube", {
                icon = "cube",
                tooltip = "Spawn Cube Hologram in front of Freecam",
            }) then
                actions.spawnQuickModel("Cube", "models/hunter/blocks/cube1x1x1.mdl")
            end
            cx = cx + cubeW + 4
        end

        -- Viewport Toggles
        local gridW = compactBar and 52 or 62
        if cx + gridW <= maxLeftX then
            if Widgets.button(ui, "tb_grid", cx, by, gridW, btnH, "Grid", {
                icon = "grid",
                active = state.grid.enabled,
                tooltip = "Toggle 3D Ground Grid (G)",
            }) then
                state.grid.enabled = not state.grid.enabled
            end
            cx = cx + gridW + 4
        end

        local axesW = compactBar and 52 or 62
        if cx + axesW <= maxLeftX then
            if Widgets.button(ui, "tb_axes", cx, by, axesW, btnH, "Axes", {
                icon = "axes",
                active = state.grid.showAxes,
                tooltip = "Toggle 3D Origin Axes",
            }) then
                state.grid.showAxes = not state.grid.showAxes
            end
            cx = cx + axesW + 4
        end

        local statsW = compactBar and 54 or 64
        if cx + statsW <= maxLeftX then
            if Widgets.button(ui, "tb_stats", cx, by, statsW, btnH, "Stats", {
                icon = "scene",
                active = state.grid.showTelemetry,
                tooltip = "Toggle Viewport Telemetry HUD",
            }) then
                state.grid.showTelemetry = not state.grid.showTelemetry
            end
            cx = cx + statsW + 4
        end

        -- History (Undo/Redo) & Upcoming Project buttons
        if cx + 40 <= maxLeftX then
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.border)
            render.drawLine(cx + 2, y + 7, cx + 2, y + h - 7)
            cx = cx + 10

            if cx + 30 <= maxLeftX then
                local undoTip = actions.canUndo
                    and ("Undo: " .. (actions.undoLabel or "Edit") .. " (Ctrl+Z)")
                    or "Undo (Nothing to undo)"
                if Widgets.button(ui, "tb_undo", cx, by, 30, btnH, nil, {
                    icon = "undo",
                    disabled = not actions.canUndo,
                    tooltip = undoTip,
                }) then
                    if actions.undo then actions.undo() end
                end
                cx = cx + 34
            end

            if cx + 30 <= maxLeftX then
                local redoTip = actions.canRedo
                    and ("Redo: " .. (actions.redoLabel or "Edit") .. " (Ctrl+Y)")
                    or "Redo (Nothing to redo)"
                if Widgets.button(ui, "tb_redo", cx, by, 30, btnH, nil, {
                    icon = "redo",
                    disabled = not actions.canRedo,
                    tooltip = redoTip,
                }) then
                    if actions.redo then actions.redo() end
                end
                cx = cx + 34
            end

            for _, item in ipairs(TOOLBAR_UPCOMING) do
                if cx + item.w > maxLeftX then break end
                Widgets.button(ui, "tb_" .. item.id, cx, by, item.w, btnH, item.label, {
                    icon = item.icon,
                    disabled = true,
                    tooltip = item.tip,
                })
                cx = cx + item.w + 4
            end
        end

        ui:popClip()
    end

    SOLACE.Toolbar = Toolbar

    -- 14.3 Right-Top Scene Explorer


    local Explorer = {
        _scrollY = 0,
        _contentH = 0,
        _viewH = 0,
        _lastCount = -1,
        _countStr = "0 nodes",
    }

    local KIND_ICONS = {
        [Constants.NODE_KINDS.ROOT]     = "scene",
        [Constants.NODE_KINDS.FOLDER]   = "layers",
        [Constants.NODE_KINDS.GROUP]    = "layers",
        [Constants.NODE_KINDS.MODEL]    = "cube",
        [Constants.NODE_KINDS.HOLOGRAM] = "holo",
        [Constants.NODE_KINDS.CAMERA]   = "camera",
        [Constants.NODE_KINDS.SPRITE]   = "sparkle",
        [Constants.NODE_KINDS.EFFECT]   = "burst",
        [Constants.NODE_KINDS.DECAL]    = "stamp",
    }

    function Explorer.draw(ui, state, sceneGraph, rect, actions, opts)
        if rect.w <= 0 or rect.h <= 0 then return end

        local count = sceneGraph:getNodeCount()
        if count ~= Explorer._lastCount then
            Explorer._lastCount = count
            Explorer._countStr = string.format("%d node%s", count, count == 1 and "" or "s")
        end
        local bx, by, bw, bh
        if opts and opts.hideFrame then
            bx, by, bw, bh = rect.x, rect.y, rect.w, rect.h
        else
            bx, by, bw, bh = Widgets.panelFrame(rect.x, rect.y, rect.w, rect.h, "Explorer", Explorer._countStr, {
                ui = ui,
                undockId = "exp_undock",
                onUndock = function()
                    if state.floatingPanels then
                        state.floatingPanels.explorer = true
                        if SOLACE.ImGui then SOLACE.ImGui.bringToFront("win_panel_explorer") end
                    end
                end,
            })
        end
        if bw < 20 or bh < 60 then return end

        local pad = Theme.Metrics.pad
        local topBarH = 56
        local listY = by + topBarH
        local listH = math.max(0, bh - topBarH)
        Explorer._viewH = listH

        -- Scroll tree area with mouse wheel
        if ui.wheelDelta ~= 0 and ui:pointInRect(bx, listY, bw, listH) then
            Explorer._scrollY = Explorer._scrollY - ui.wheelDelta * 24
        end
        local maxScroll = math.max(0, Explorer._contentH - listH)
        Explorer._scrollY = math.clamp(Explorer._scrollY, 0, maxScroll)

        ui:pushClip(bx, by, bw, bh)

        -- Search box
        Widgets.textBox(
            ui,
            "explorer_search",
            bx + pad,
            by + 6,
            bw - pad * 2,
            22,
            function() return state.explorerSearch end,
            function(v) state.explorerSearch = v end,
            "Search scene hierarchy...",
            "Filter Scene IR nodes by name or kind"
        )

        -- Quick hierarchy toolbar strip
        local actY = by + 32
        local gap = 4
        local btnW = math.floor((bw - pad * 2 - gap * 3) / 4)
        local selNode = sceneGraph:getSelectedNode()
        local canDelete = (selNode ~= nil and selNode.id ~= sceneGraph:getRootId())
        local compactExp = (btnW < 52)

        if Widgets.button(ui, "exp_add_grp", bx + pad, actY, btnW, 20, compactExp and "+G" or "+Grp", {
            tooltip = "Create Group under selected container or root",
        }) then
            actions.spawnGroup()
        end
        if Widgets.button(ui, "exp_add_fld", bx + pad + (btnW + gap), actY, btnW, 20, compactExp and "+F" or "+Fld", {
            tooltip = "Create Folder under selected container or root",
        }) then
            actions.spawnFolder()
        end
        if Widgets.button(ui, "exp_focus", bx + pad + (btnW + gap) * 2, actY, btnW, 20, compactExp and "Foc" or "Focus", {
            disabled = not canDelete,
            tooltip = "Focus Freecam on selected object (F)",
        }) then
            actions.focusSelected()
        end
        if Widgets.button(ui, "exp_del", bx + pad + (btnW + gap) * 3, actY, btnW, 20, "Del", {
            disabled = not canDelete,
            tooltip = "Delete selected object(s) (Delete)",
        }) then
            actions.deleteSelected()
        end

        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.border)
        render.drawLine(bx, listY - 1, bx + bw, listY - 1)

        -- Scrollable tree body
        ui:pushClip(bx, listY, bw, listH)

        local rows = sceneGraph:getFlatHierarchy(state.explorerSearch)
        local rowH = Theme.Metrics.rowH
        local primaryId = sceneGraph:getSelectedId()
        local curY = listY + 4 - Explorer._scrollY
        local startY = curY

        for _, item in ipairs(rows) do
            local node = item.node
            if curY + rowH >= listY and curY <= listY + listH then
                local indent = 8 + item.depth * 14
                local isSel = sceneGraph:isSelected(node.id)
                local isPrimary = (node.id == primaryId)
                local rowId = "exp_row_" .. node.id
                local eyeSize = 18
                local rowW = bw - (maxScroll > 0 and 14 or 4)
                local eyeX = bx + bw - eyeSize - (maxScroll > 0 and 14 or 6)

                local hot, _ = ui:hitTest(rowId, bx + 2, curY, rowW, rowH)

                if isSel then
                    Theme.setColor(Theme.C.bgSelectionRow, isPrimary and 120 or 75)
                    render.drawRectFast(bx + 2, curY, rowW, rowH)
                    Theme.setColor(isPrimary and Theme.C.accentBright or Theme.C.accent)
                    render.drawRectFast(bx + 2, curY, 2, rowH)
                elseif hot then
                    Theme.setColor(Theme.C.bgControlHover, 140)
                    render.drawRectFast(bx + 2, curY, rowW, rowH)
                end

                -- Expand / collapse chevron
                local handledClick = false
                if item.hasChildren then
                    local chevX = bx + indent
                    local chevHot, _ = ui:hitTest("exp_chev_" .. node.id, chevX - 2, curY, 16, rowH)
                    Theme.drawIcon(
                        node.expanded and "chevronDown" or "chevronRight",
                        chevX,
                        curY + (rowH - 12) * 0.5,
                        12,
                        chevHot and Theme.C.textPrimary or Theme.C.textMuted
                    )
                    if chevHot and ui.leftPressed then
                        node.expanded = not node.expanded
                        sceneGraph:bumpHierarchyVersion()
                        handledClick = true
                    end
                end

                -- Visibility toggle eye button (non-root nodes)
                if node.id ~= sceneGraph:getRootId() then
                    local eyeHot, _ = ui:hitTest("exp_eye_" .. node.id, eyeX, curY, eyeSize, rowH)
                    if eyeHot then
                        ui:setTooltip(node.visible and "Hide object" or "Show object")
                    end
                    Theme.drawIcon(
                        "eye",
                        eyeX + 2,
                        curY + (rowH - 13) * 0.5,
                        13,
                        node.visible and (eyeHot and Theme.C.textPrimary or Theme.C.textSecondary) or Theme.C.textDisabled
                    )
                    if eyeHot and ui.leftPressed then
                        sceneGraph:toggleNodeVisible(node.id)
                        handledClick = true
                    end
                end

                -- Row selection (supports Ctrl+Click multi-select)
                if hot and ui.leftPressed and not handledClick then
                    local ctrl = hasPermission("input") and input.isControlDown() or false
                    if ctrl then
                        sceneGraph:toggleSelectedId(node.id)
                    else
                        sceneGraph:setSelectedId(node.id)
                    end
                end

                local iconX = bx + indent + 15
                local iconName = KIND_ICONS[node.kind] or "cube"
                local textAlpha = node.visible and 255 or 120
                Theme.drawIcon(
                    iconName,
                    iconX,
                    curY + (rowH - 13) * 0.5,
                    13,
                    isSel and Theme.C.accentBright or Theme.C.textSecondary,
                    textAlpha
                )

                Theme.setFont(isSel and Theme.Fonts.BodyBold or Theme.Fonts.Body)
                Theme.setColor(isSel and Theme.C.textPrimary or Theme.C.textSecondary, textAlpha)
                render.drawSimpleText(iconX + 18, curY + rowH * 0.5, node.name, TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)

                if eyeX - (iconX + 18) >= 128 then
                    Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
                    Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textMuted, textAlpha)
                    render.drawSimpleText(eyeX - 6, curY + rowH * 0.5, node.kind, TEXT_ALIGN.RIGHT, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)
                end
            end
            curY = curY + rowH
        end

        Explorer._contentH = (curY - startY) + 8
        maxScroll = math.max(0, Explorer._contentH - listH)

        if maxScroll > 0 then
            Explorer._scrollY = Widgets.scrollbar(
                ui, "exp_sb",
                bx + bw - 8, listY + 2, 6, listH - 4,
                Explorer._scrollY, Explorer._contentH, listH
            )
        end

        ui:popClip()
        ui:popClip()
    end

    SOLACE.Explorer = Explorer

    -- 14.4 Right-Bottom Properties Inspector


    local Properties = {
        _scrollY = 0,
        _contentH = 0,
        _viewH = 0,
        _selCountLast = -1,
        _selCountLabel = "0 selected",
        _xformCache = {},
        _rtCacheTime = -1,
        _rtCpuStr = "",
        _rtRamStr = "",
        _rtHolosStr = "",
        _rtCalcPerm = false,
        _rtHoloPerm = false,
    }

    local function fmtVecCached(cache, key, v, dec)
        local x, y, z = v[1], v[2], v[3]
        local c = cache[key]
        if c and c.x == x and c.y == y and c.z == z and c.dec == dec then
            return c.str
        end
        local str = Utils.fmtVec3(v, dec)
        cache[key] = { x = x, y = y, z = z, dec = dec, str = str }
        return str
    end

    function Properties.draw(ui, state, sceneGraph, runtime, camera, rect, actions, opts)
        if rect.w <= 0 or rect.h <= 0 then return end

        local sel = sceneGraph:getSelectedNode()
        local selCount = #sceneGraph:getSelectedIds()
        local rightLabel
        if selCount > 1 then
            if selCount ~= Properties._selCountLast then
                Properties._selCountLast = selCount
                Properties._selCountLabel = selCount .. " selected"
            end
            rightLabel = Properties._selCountLabel
        else
            rightLabel = sel and sel.name or "No Selection"
        end
        local bx, by, bw, bh
        if opts and opts.hideFrame then
            bx, by, bw, bh = rect.x, rect.y, rect.w, rect.h
        else
            bx, by, bw, bh = Widgets.panelFrame(rect.x, rect.y, rect.w, rect.h, "Properties", rightLabel, {
                ui = ui,
                undockId = "prop_undock",
                onUndock = function()
                    if state.floatingPanels then
                        state.floatingPanels.properties = true
                        if SOLACE.ImGui then SOLACE.ImGui.bringToFront("win_panel_properties") end
                    end
                end,
            })
        end
        if bw < 20 or bh < 40 then return end
        Properties._viewH = bh

        if ui.wheelDelta ~= 0 and ui:pointInRect(bx, by, bw, bh) then
            Properties._scrollY = Properties._scrollY - ui.wheelDelta * 28
        end
        local maxScroll = math.max(0, Properties._contentH - bh)
        Properties._scrollY = math.clamp(Properties._scrollY, 0, maxScroll)

        ui:pushClip(bx, by, bw, bh)

        local pad = Theme.Metrics.pad
        local rowH = Theme.Metrics.controlH
        local headerW = bw - (maxScroll > 0 and 12 or 0)
        local innerW = bw - pad * 2 - (maxScroll > 0 and 12 or 0)
        local curY = by + pad - Properties._scrollY
        local startY = curY
        local visBottom = by + bh

        -- 1. Selected Object
        if curY + 22 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
            state.sections.selection = Widgets.sectionHeader(ui, "sec_selection", bx, curY, headerW, 22, "Selection", state.sections.selection)
        end
        curY = curY + 26
        if state.sections.selection then
            if sel then
                local isRoot = (sel.id == sceneGraph:getRootId())

                if not isRoot then
                    local nameGetter = function() return sel.name end
                    local nameSetter = function(v) sceneGraph:renameNode(sel.id, v) end
                    if curY + 22 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                        Widgets.textBox(
                            ui,
                            "prop_node_name_" .. sel.id,
                            bx + pad,
                            curY,
                            innerW,
                            22,
                            nameGetter,
                            nameSetter,
                            "Object name...",
                            "Rename selected object"
                        )
                    else
                        ui:registerTextInput("prop_node_name_" .. sel.id, nameGetter, nameSetter)
                    end
                    curY = curY + 26
                end

                if curY + 18 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    Widgets.keyValueRow(bx + pad, curY, innerW, 18, "Kind", sel.kind, Theme.C.accentBright)
                end
                curY = curY + 19
                if curY + 18 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    Widgets.keyValueRow(bx + pad, curY, innerW, 18, "IR ID", sel.id, Theme.C.textMuted)
                end
                curY = curY + 19

                if sel.appearance.model and sel.appearance.model ~= "" then
                    if curY + 18 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                        local shortMdl = string.getFileFromFilename(sel.appearance.model) or sel.appearance.model
                        Widgets.keyValueRow(bx + pad, curY, innerW, 18, "Model", shortMdl, Theme.C.textSecondary)
                    end
                    curY = curY + 19
                elseif sel.appearance.material and sel.appearance.material ~= "" then
                    if curY + 18 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                        Widgets.keyValueRow(bx + pad, curY, innerW, 18, "Material", sel.appearance.material, Theme.C.textSecondary)
                    end
                    curY = curY + 19
                end

                if not isRoot then
                    local xc = Properties._xformCache
                    if xc._id ~= sel.id then
                        Properties._xformCache = { _id = sel.id }
                        xc = Properties._xformCache
                    end
                    if curY + 18 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                        Widgets.keyValueRow(bx + pad, curY, innerW, 18, "Position", fmtVecCached(xc, "pos", sel.transform.pos, 1))
                    end
                    curY = curY + 19
                    if curY + 18 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                        Widgets.keyValueRow(bx + pad, curY, innerW, 18, "Rotation", fmtVecCached(xc, "ang", sel.transform.ang, 1))
                    end
                    curY = curY + 19
                    if curY + 18 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                        Widgets.keyValueRow(bx + pad, curY, innerW, 18, "Scale", fmtVecCached(xc, "scale", sel.transform.scale, 2))
                    end
                    curY = curY + 22

                    local entry = runtime:getEntry(sel.id)
                    if entry and entry.errorMsg then
                        if curY + 18 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                            Widgets.keyValueRow(bx + pad, curY, innerW, 18, "Runtime", entry.errorMsg, Theme.C.danger)
                        end
                        curY = curY + 20
                    end

                    if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                        local thirdW = math.floor((innerW - 8) / 3)
                        if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_vis", bx + pad, curY, thirdW, rowH, sel.visible and "Visible" or "Hidden", {
                            active = sel.visible,
                            tooltip = "Toggle object visibility",
                        }) then
                            sceneGraph:toggleNodeVisible(sel.id)
                        end
                        if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_focus", bx + pad + thirdW + 4, curY, thirdW, rowH, "Focus", {
                            tooltip = "Move Freecam to frame this object (F)",
                        }) then
                            actions.focusSelected()
                        end
                        if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_del", bx + pad + (thirdW + 4) * 2, curY, innerW - (thirdW + 4) * 2, rowH, "Delete", {
                            tooltip = "Delete selected object(s) (Del)",
                        }) then
                            actions.deleteSelected()
                        end
                    end
                    curY = curY + rowH + 6

                    if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                        local thirdW = math.floor((innerW - 8) / 3)
                        if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_tool_move", bx + pad, curY, thirdW, rowH, "Move", {
                            active = (state.activeTool == "move"),
                            tooltip = "Switch to 3D Move Gizmo (2)",
                        }) then
                            if actions.setTool then actions.setTool("move") else state.activeTool = "move" end
                        end
                        if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_tool_rot", bx + pad + thirdW + 4, curY, thirdW, rowH, "Rotate", {
                            active = (state.activeTool == "rotate"),
                            tooltip = "Switch to 3D Rotate Gizmo (E / 3)",
                        }) then
                            if actions.setTool then actions.setTool("rotate") else state.activeTool = "rotate" end
                        end
                        if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_tool_scl", bx + pad + (thirdW + 4) * 2, curY, innerW - (thirdW + 4) * 2, rowH, "Scale", {
                            active = (state.activeTool == "scale"),
                            tooltip = "Switch to 3D Scale Gizmo (R / 4)",
                        }) then
                            if actions.setTool then actions.setTool("scale") else state.activeTool = "scale" end
                        end
                    end
                    curY = curY + rowH + 6
                end

                local nh = 64
                if curY + nh >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    nh = Widgets.phaseNotice(
                        bx + pad, curY, innerW,
                        "Numeric Transforms",
                        { "Use 3D Move/Rotate/Scale gizmos;", "numeric edit boxes arrive in Phase 4." },
                        "Phase 4"
                    )
                end
                curY = curY + nh + 8
            else
                if curY + 18 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    Widgets.keyValueRow(bx + pad, curY, innerW, 18, "Selection", "None (Click object in viewport)", Theme.C.textMuted)
                end
                curY = curY + 24
            end
        end
