        -- 2. Freecam Camera (live)
        if curY + 22 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
            state.sections.camera = Widgets.sectionHeader(ui, "sec_camera", bx, curY, headerW, 22, "Freecam Camera", state.sections.camera)
        end
        curY = curY + 26
        if state.sections.camera then
            local c = state.camera
            if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                local spd, spdChanged = Widgets.slider(
                    ui, "prop_cam_spd", bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                    "Move Speed", c.speed, Constants.CAMERA_DEFAULTS.minSpeed, Constants.CAMERA_DEFAULTS.maxSpeed, 10, "%.0f u/s",
                    "Freecam base movement speed (Mouse wheel in viewport adjusts this)"
                )
                if spdChanged then c.speed = spd end
            end
            curY = curY + rowH + 4

            if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                local bst, bstChanged = Widgets.slider(
                    ui, "prop_cam_bst", bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                    "Shift Boost", c.boostMult, 1.5, 6.0, 0.25, "x%.2f",
                    "Speed multiplier while holding Shift"
                )
                if bstChanged then c.boostMult = bst end
            end
            curY = curY + rowH + 4

            if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                local sens, sensChanged = Widgets.slider(
                    ui, "prop_cam_sens", bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                    "Mouse Sens", c.sensitivity, 0.04, 0.50, 0.01, "%.2f",
                    "Mouse look sensitivity while holding Right Mouse Button"
                )
                if sensChanged then c.sensitivity = sens end
            end
            curY = curY + rowH + 4

            if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                local fov, fovChanged = Widgets.slider(
                    ui, "prop_cam_fov", bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                    "Field of View", c.fov, 45, 115, 1, "%.0f deg",
                    "Viewport camera vertical field of view"
                )
                if fovChanged then c.fov = fov end
            end
            curY = curY + rowH + 4

            if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                local sm, smChanged = Widgets.slider(
                    ui, "prop_cam_sm", bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                    "Smoothing", c.smoothing, 4, 40, 1, "%.0f",
                    "Camera motion damping (40 = crisp / instant)"
                )
                if smChanged then c.smoothing = sm end
            end
            curY = curY + rowH + 4

            if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_cam_reset", bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH, "Snap Camera to Player", {
                    icon = "camera",
                    tooltip = "Move Freecam back to your player's eye position",
                }) then
                    camera:resetToPlayer()
                end
            end
            curY = curY + rowH + 8
        end

        -- 3. Viewport Grid (live)
        if curY + 22 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
            state.sections.grid = Widgets.sectionHeader(ui, "sec_grid", bx, curY, headerW, 22, "Viewport Grid", state.sections.grid)
        end
        curY = curY + 26
        if state.sections.grid then
            local g = state.grid

            if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_grid_en", bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                    "Ground Grid: " .. (g.enabled and "Visible" or "Hidden"),
                    { active = g.enabled, icon = "grid", tooltip = "Toggle 3D reference grid (Hotkey: G)" }
                ) then
                    g.enabled = not g.enabled
                end
            end
            curY = curY + rowH + 4

            if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                local prevSp, nextSp = Widgets.stepper(
                    ui, "prop_grid_sp", bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                    "Cell Spacing", tostring(g.spacing) .. " u",
                    "Grid cell size in Hammer units"
                )
                if prevSp then g.spacing = Utils.cycleOption(Constants.GRID_SPACING_OPTIONS, g.spacing, -1) end
                if nextSp then g.spacing = Utils.cycleOption(Constants.GRID_SPACING_OPTIONS, g.spacing, 1) end
            end
            curY = curY + rowH + 4

            if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                local newExt, extChanged = Widgets.slider(
                    ui, "prop_grid_ext", bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                    "Grid Radius", g.halfExtents, 4, 24, 1, "%.0f cells",
                    "Number of grid cells from origin in each direction"
                )
                if extChanged then g.halfExtents = newExt end
            end
            curY = curY + rowH + 8
        end

        -- 4. Studio Origin & HUD
        if curY + 22 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
            state.sections.origin = Widgets.sectionHeader(ui, "sec_origin", bx, curY, headerW, 22, "Studio Origin & HUD", state.sections.origin)
        end
        curY = curY + 26
        if state.sections.origin then
            local g = state.grid
            if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                local prevOr, nextOr = Widgets.stepper(
                    ui, "prop_origin_mode", bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                    "Origin Anchor", string.upper(g.originMode),
                    "Center studio grid on Chip, Player, or World (0,0,0)"
                )
                if prevOr then
                    g.originMode = Utils.cycleOption(Constants.ORIGIN_MODES, g.originMode, -1)
                    actions.resolveOrigin()
                end
                if nextOr then
                    g.originMode = Utils.cycleOption(Constants.ORIGIN_MODES, g.originMode, 1)
                    actions.resolveOrigin()
                end
            end
            curY = curY + rowH + 4

            if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                local halfW = math.floor((innerW - 4) * 0.5)
                if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_triad", bx + pad, curY, halfW, rowH, "3D Triad", {
                    active = g.showOrientationGizmo,
                    tooltip = "Toggle top-right 3D axis orientation widget",
                }) then
                    g.showOrientationGizmo = not g.showOrientationGizmo
                end
                if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_telem", bx + pad + halfW + 4, curY, innerW - halfW - 4, rowH, "Telemetry", {
                    active = g.showTelemetry,
                    tooltip = "Toggle bottom-left performance telemetry pill",
                }) then
                    g.showTelemetry = not g.showTelemetry
                end
            end
            curY = curY + rowH + 8
        end

        -- 5. StarfallEx Quotas & Permissions
        if curY + 22 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
            state.sections.quotas = Widgets.sectionHeader(ui, "sec_quotas", bx, curY, headerW, 22, "StarfallEx Runtime", state.sections.quotas)
        end
        curY = curY + 26
        if state.sections.quotas then
            if curY + 95 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                local now = ui._frameTime
                if now - Properties._rtCacheTime >= 0.2 or Properties._rtCpuStr == "" then
                    Properties._rtCacheTime = now
                    local cpuAvgFn = cpuAverage or quotaAverage
                    local cpuMaxFn = cpuMax or quotaMax
                    local cpuMs = cpuAvgFn and (cpuAvgFn() * 1000) or 0
                    local maxMs = cpuMaxFn and (cpuMaxFn() * 1000) or 15
                    local ramMb = ramUsed and (ramUsed() / 1024) or 0
                    local holosLeft = (hologram and hologram.hologramsLeft) and hologram.hologramsLeft() or 0
                    Properties._rtCpuStr = string.format("%.2f / %.1f ms", cpuMs, maxMs)
                    Properties._rtRamStr = string.format("%.1f MB", ramMb)
                    Properties._rtHolosStr = tostring(holosLeft)
                    Properties._rtCalcPerm = hasPermission("render.calcview")
                    Properties._rtHoloPerm = hasPermission("hologram.create")
                end
                if curY + 18 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    Widgets.keyValueRow(bx + pad, curY, innerW, 18, "CPU Avg / Cap", Properties._rtCpuStr)
                end
                curY = curY + 19
                if curY + 18 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    Widgets.keyValueRow(bx + pad, curY, innerW, 18, "RAM Used", Properties._rtRamStr)
                end
                curY = curY + 19
                if curY + 18 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    Widgets.keyValueRow(bx + pad, curY, innerW, 18, "Holograms Left", Properties._rtHolosStr)
                end
                curY = curY + 19
                if curY + 18 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    Widgets.keyValueRow(bx + pad, curY, innerW, 18, "render.calcview", Properties._rtCalcPerm and "OK" or "Denied",
                        Properties._rtCalcPerm and Theme.C.success or Theme.C.danger)
                end
                curY = curY + 19
                if curY + 18 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    Widgets.keyValueRow(bx + pad, curY, innerW, 18, "hologram.create", Properties._rtHoloPerm and "OK" or "Pending",
                        Properties._rtHoloPerm and Theme.C.success or Theme.C.warning)
                end
                curY = curY + 24
            else
                curY = curY + 100
            end
        end

        Properties._contentH = (curY - startY) + pad
        maxScroll = math.max(0, Properties._contentH - bh)

        if maxScroll > 0 then
            Properties._scrollY = Widgets.scrollbar(
                ui, "prop_sb",
                bx + bw - 8, by + 2, 6, bh - 4,
                Properties._scrollY, Properties._contentH, bh
            )
        end

        ui:popClip()
    end

    SOLACE.Properties = Properties
end)(SOLACE) end

-- ============================================================================
-- SECTION 15: Timeline
-- ============================================================================
do (function(SOLACE)
    local Constants = SOLACE.Constants
    local Utils = SOLACE.Utils
    local Theme = SOLACE.Theme
    local Widgets = SOLACE.Widgets



    local Timeline = {
        _lastFrame = -1,
        _lastTotal = -1,
        _lastFps = -1,
        _badgeStr = "",
    }
    local SEC_LABELS = {}
    for s = 0, 120 do
        SEC_LABELS[s] = tostring(s) .. "s"
    end

    local function cycleFps(current, dir)
        local opts = Constants.FPS_OPTIONS
        local idx = 2
        for i, v in ipairs(opts) do
            if v == current then idx = i break end
        end
        idx = ((idx - 1 + dir) % #opts) + 1
        return opts[idx]
    end

    function Timeline.draw(ui, state, sceneGraph, rect, opts)
        if rect.w <= 0 or rect.h <= 0 then return end

        local tl = state.timeline
        local frameIdx = math.floor(tl.currentTime * tl.fps + 0.5)
        local totalFrames = math.floor(tl.duration * tl.fps + 0.5)
        if frameIdx ~= Timeline._lastFrame or totalFrames ~= Timeline._lastTotal or tl.fps ~= Timeline._lastFps then
            Timeline._lastFrame = frameIdx
            Timeline._lastTotal = totalFrames
            Timeline._lastFps = tl.fps
            Timeline._badgeStr = string.format("%s / %s  |  F %d / %d (%d FPS)",
                Utils.formatTimecode(tl.currentTime),
                Utils.formatTimecode(tl.duration),
                frameIdx,
                totalFrames,
                tl.fps
            )
        end

        local bx, by, bw, bh
        if opts and opts.hideFrame then
            bx, by, bw, bh = rect.x, rect.y, rect.w, rect.h
        else
            bx, by, bw, bh = Widgets.panelFrame(rect.x, rect.y, rect.w, rect.h, "Timeline", Timeline._badgeStr, {
                ui = ui,
                undockId = "tl_undock",
                onUndock = function()
                    if state.floatingPanels then
                        state.floatingPanels.timeline = true
                        if SOLACE.ImGui then SOLACE.ImGui.bringToFront("win_panel_timeline") end
                    end
                end,
            })
        end
        if bw < 160 or bh < 60 then return end

        ui:pushClip(bx, by, bw, bh)

        -- Transport & ruler strip
        local stripH = 28
        local headerColW = math.clamp(math.floor(bw * 0.22), 198, 224)

        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.bgPanelAlt)
        render.drawRectFast(bx, by, bw, stripH)
        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.border)
        render.drawLine(bx, by + stripH - 1, bx + bw, by + stripH - 1)
        render.drawLine(bx + headerColW, by, bx + headerColW, by + bh)

        -- Left transport controls
        local tx = bx + 6
        local ty = by + 3
        local btnH = 22

        Widgets.button(ui, "tl_play", tx, ty, 26, btnH, nil, {
            icon = "play", disabled = true, tooltip = "Play Sequence — Arriving in Phase 8",
        })
        tx = tx + 29
        Widgets.button(ui, "tl_stop", tx, ty, 26, btnH, nil, {
            icon = "stop", disabled = true, tooltip = "Stop Sequence — Arriving in Phase 8",
        })
        tx = tx + 32

        if Widgets.button(ui, "tl_fps", tx, ty, 52, btnH, tostring(tl.fps) .. "fps", {
            tooltip = "Cycle Timeline Frame Rate (24 / 30 / 60 FPS)",
        }) then
            tl.fps = cycleFps(tl.fps, 1)
        end
        tx = tx + 56

        if Widgets.button(ui, "tl_dur_minus", tx, ty, 20, btnH, "-", {
            tooltip = "Decrease sequence duration (-5s)",
        }) then
            tl.duration = math.max(2, tl.duration - 5)
            tl.currentTime = math.min(tl.currentTime, tl.duration)
        end
        tx = tx + 21
        if Widgets.button(ui, "tl_dur_plus", tx, ty, 20, btnH, "+", {
            tooltip = "Increase sequence duration (+5s)",
        }) then
            tl.duration = math.min(120, tl.duration + 5)
        end
        tx = tx + 24

        if Widgets.button(ui, "tl_zoom_out", tx, ty, 20, btnH, "z-", {
            tooltip = "Zoom Out Timeline",
        }) then
            tl.zoom = math.clamp(tl.zoom * 0.8, 25, 320)
        end
        tx = tx + 21
        if Widgets.button(ui, "tl_zoom_in", tx, ty, 20, btnH, "z+", {
            tooltip = "Zoom In Timeline",
        }) then
            tl.zoom = math.clamp(tl.zoom * 1.25, 25, 320)
        end

        -- Right interactive Time Ruler
        local rulerX = bx + headerColW + 1
        local rulerW = math.max(40, bw - headerColW - 1)
        local rulerY = by
        local trackAreaY = by + stripH
        local trackAreaH = math.max(0, bh - stripH)

        -- Wheel zoom inside ruler/tracks
        if ui.wheelDelta ~= 0 and ui:pointInRect(rulerX, by, rulerW, bh) and ui:isMouseInClip() then
            local factor = ui.wheelDelta > 0 and 1.15 or 0.87
            tl.zoom = math.clamp(tl.zoom * factor, 25, 320)
        end

        -- Ruler scrubbing (click or drag anywhere on ruler)
        local rulerHot, rulerCap = ui:hitTest("tl_ruler", rulerX, rulerY, rulerW, stripH)
        if rulerHot then
            ui:setTooltip("Click or drag to scrub playhead (Mouse wheel to zoom)")
            if ui.leftPressed then
                ui:capture("tl_ruler")
            end
        end
        if rulerCap and ui.leftDown then
            local t = tl.scrollTime + (ui.mx - rulerX - 10) / tl.zoom
            tl.currentTime = math.clamp(t, 0, tl.duration)
        end

        ui:pushClip(rulerX, by, rulerW, bh)

        -- Ruler background & ticks
        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.bgInput)
        render.drawRectFast(rulerX, rulerY, rulerW, stripH - 1)

        local originPX = rulerX + 10 - tl.scrollTime * tl.zoom
        local durPX = originPX + tl.duration * tl.zoom

        -- Active sequence span highlight
        local spanX1 = math.max(rulerX, originPX)
        local spanX2 = math.min(rulerX + rulerW, durPX)
        if spanX2 > spanX1 then
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.bgPanel, 220)
            render.drawRectFast(spanX1, trackAreaY, spanX2 - spanX1, trackAreaH)
        end

        local secStep = tl.zoom < 50 and 2 or 1
        local subDiv = tl.zoom >= 110 and 4 or (tl.zoom >= 65 and 2 or 1)
        local minSec = math.max(0, math.floor((rulerX - 20 - originPX) / tl.zoom))
        local maxSec = math.min(math.ceil(tl.duration), math.ceil((rulerX + rulerW + 20 - originPX) / tl.zoom))

        Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
        for sec = minSec, maxSec do
            local sx = originPX + sec * tl.zoom
            if sec % secStep == 0 then
                Theme.setColor(Theme.C.borderLight)
                render.drawLine(sx, rulerY + 12, sx, rulerY + stripH - 2)
                Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textSecondary)
                render.drawSimpleText(sx + 3, rulerY + 4, SEC_LABELS[sec] or (sec .. "s"), TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.TOP)

                -- Vertical guide line into track body
                Theme.setColor(Theme.C.border, 110)
                render.drawLine(sx, trackAreaY, sx, trackAreaY + trackAreaH)
            else
                Theme.setColor(Theme.C.border)
                render.drawLine(sx, rulerY + 18, sx, rulerY + stripH - 2)
            end

            if subDiv > 1 and sec < tl.duration then
                for sub = 1, subDiv - 1 do
                    local subX = sx + (sub / subDiv) * tl.zoom
                    Theme.setColor(Theme.C.border, 140)
                    render.drawLine(subX, rulerY + 21, subX, rulerY + stripH - 2)
                end
            end
        end

        -- End-of-sequence marker
        if durPX >= rulerX and durPX <= rulerX + rulerW then
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.warning, 160)
            render.drawLine(durPX, rulerY, durPX, by + bh)
        end

        -- Playhead scrubber line + handle
        local playX = math.floor(originPX + tl.currentTime * tl.zoom)
        if playX >= rulerX and playX <= rulerX + rulerW then
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.accent)
            render.drawLine(playX, rulerY + 4, playX, by + bh)
            render.drawRectFast(playX - 5, rulerY + 3, 11, 10)
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textPrimary)
            render.drawRectOutline(playX - 5, rulerY + 3, 11, 10, 1)
        end

        -- Track rows preview (mirrors current IR nodes)
        local selId = sceneGraph:getSelectedId()
        local rows = sceneGraph:getFlatHierarchy("")
        local rowY = trackAreaY + 4
        local rowH = 22
        for _, item in ipairs(rows) do
            if item.node.id ~= sceneGraph:getRootId() and rowY + rowH <= by + bh - 4 then
                local isSel = (item.node.id == selId)
                if isSel then
                    Theme.setColor(Theme.C.accentSoft, 80)
                    render.drawRectFast(rulerX, rowY, rulerW, rowH)
                end
                Theme.setColor(Theme.C.border, 90)
                render.drawLine(rulerX, rowY + rowH, rulerX + rulerW, rowY + rowH)
                rowY = rowY + rowH
            end
        end

        if trackAreaH >= 48 then
            Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textMuted)
            render.drawSimpleText(
                rulerX + 12,
                by + bh - 14,
                "Keyframe tracks, interpolation & easing curves arrive in Phase 8 (Ruler scrubbing is live).",
                TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT,
                TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER
            )
        end

        ui:popClip()

        -- Left track label list (clipped to headerColW)
        ui:pushClip(bx, trackAreaY, headerColW, trackAreaH)
        local lRowY = trackAreaY + 4
        for _, item in ipairs(rows) do
            if item.node.id ~= sceneGraph:getRootId() and lRowY + rowH <= by + bh - 4 then
                local isSel = (item.node.id == selId)
                if isSel then
                    Theme.setColor(Theme.C.bgSelectionRow)
                    render.drawRectFast(bx + 2, lRowY, headerColW - 4, rowH)
                end
                Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Body)
                Theme.setColor(isSel and Theme.C.textPrimary or Theme.C.textSecondary)
                render.drawSimpleText(bx + 10, lRowY + rowH * 0.5, item.node.name, TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)

                Theme.setColor(Theme.C.border, 90)
                render.drawLine(bx, lRowY + rowH, bx + headerColW, lRowY + rowH)
                lRowY = lRowY + rowH
            end
        end
        ui:popClip()

        ui:popClip()
    end

    SOLACE.Timeline = Timeline
end)(SOLACE) end

-- ============================================================================
-- SECTION 16: Animation system (Phase 8)
-- ============================================================================
do (function(SOLACE)
    -- Reserved for Phase 8: Keyframe Track Evaluation, Interpolation & Easing Curves.
    local Animation = {
        ready = false,
        phase = 8,
    }

    SOLACE.Animation = Animation
end)(SOLACE) end

-- ============================================================================
-- SECTION 17: Asset browser
-- ============================================================================
do (function(SOLACE)
    local Constants = SOLACE.Constants
    local Utils = SOLACE.Utils
    local Theme = SOLACE.Theme
    local Widgets = SOLACE.Widgets



    local Toolbox = {
        _scrollY = 0,
        _contentH = 0,
        _viewH = 0,
        _lastQuery = nil,
        _filteredModels = nil,
        _filteredSprites = nil,
        _lastCustomPath = nil,
        _lastCustomOk = false,
        _lastCustomName = "Model",
    }

    local function getFilteredModels(q)
        if Toolbox._lastQuery == q and Toolbox._filteredModels then
            return Toolbox._filteredModels
        end
        local list = {}
        for idx, item in ipairs(Constants.STARTER_MODELS) do
            local matches = (q == "")
                or string.find(string.lower(item.name), q, 1, true) ~= nil
                or string.find(string.lower(item.model), q, 1, true) ~= nil
            if matches and Utils.checkModelMounted(item.model) then
                list[#list + 1] = {
                    id = "tbx_mdl_" .. idx,
                    name = item.name,
                    model = item.model,
                    tip = "Spawn " .. item.model .. " in front of Freecam",
                }
            end
        end
        Toolbox._lastQuery = q
        Toolbox._filteredModels = list
        Toolbox._filteredSprites = nil
        return list
    end

    local function getFilteredSprites(q)
        if Toolbox._lastQuery == q and Toolbox._filteredSprites then
            return Toolbox._filteredSprites
        end
        local list = {}
        for idx, item in ipairs(Constants.STARTER_SPRITES) do
            if q == "" or string.find(string.lower(item.name), q, 1, true) ~= nil then
                list[#list + 1] = {
                    id = "tbx_spr_" .. idx,
                    name = item.name,
                    material = item.material,
                    tip = "Spawn 3D Sprite (" .. item.material .. ")",
                }
            end
        end
        Toolbox._lastQuery = q
        Toolbox._filteredSprites = list
        return list
    end

    function Toolbox.draw(ui, state, rect, actions, opts)
        if rect.w <= 0 or rect.h <= 0 then return end

        local bx, by, bw, bh
        if opts and opts.hideFrame then
            bx, by, bw, bh = rect.x, rect.y, rect.w, rect.h
        else
            bx, by, bw, bh = Widgets.panelFrame(rect.x, rect.y, rect.w, rect.h, "Toolbox", "Create", {
                ui = ui,
                undockId = "tbx_undock",
                onUndock = function()
                    if state.floatingPanels then
                        state.floatingPanels.toolbox = true
                        if SOLACE.ImGui then SOLACE.ImGui.bringToFront("win_panel_toolbox") end
                    end
                end,
            })
        end
        if bw < 20 or bh < 40 then return end
        Toolbox._viewH = bh

        -- Scroll body with wheel when mouse inside Toolbox
        if ui.wheelDelta ~= 0 and ui:pointInRect(bx, by, bw, bh) then
            Toolbox._scrollY = Toolbox._scrollY - ui.wheelDelta * 26
        end
        local maxScroll = math.max(0, Toolbox._contentH - bh)
        Toolbox._scrollY = math.clamp(Toolbox._scrollY, 0, maxScroll)

        ui:pushClip(bx, by, bw, bh)

        local pad = Theme.Metrics.pad
        local innerW = bw - pad * 2 - (maxScroll > 0 and 10 or 0)
        local curY = by + pad - Toolbox._scrollY
        local startY = curY
        local visBottom = by + bh

        -- Search input
        local searchGetter = function() return state.toolboxSearch end
        local searchSetter = function(v) state.toolboxSearch = v end
        if curY + 24 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
            Widgets.textBox(
                ui,
                "toolbox_search",
                bx + pad,
                curY,
                innerW,
                24,
                searchGetter,
                searchSetter,
                "Filter assets...",
                "Filter available models and sprites"
            )
        else
            ui:registerTextInput("toolbox_search", searchGetter, searchSetter)
        end
        curY = curY + 30

        -- Category buttons (2 columns when wide enough)
        local cols = innerW >= 196 and 2 or 1
        local gap = 4
        local cellW = math.floor((innerW - gap * (cols - 1)) / cols)
        local cellH = 24
        local activeCatInfo = Constants.ASSET_CATEGORIES[1]

        for i, cat in ipairs(Constants.ASSET_CATEGORIES) do
            local col = (i - 1) % cols
            local row = math.floor((i - 1) / cols)
            local cx = bx + pad + col * (cellW + gap)
            local cy = curY + row * (cellH + gap)
            local isSelected = (state.activeToolboxCategory == cat.id)
            if isSelected then activeCatInfo = cat end

            if cy + cellH >= by and cy <= visBottom then
                if Widgets.button(ui, "tbx_cat_" .. cat.id, cx, cy, cellW, cellH, cat.label, {
                    icon = cat.icon,
                    active = isSelected,
                    tooltip = cat.desc,
                }) then
                    state.activeToolboxCategory = cat.id
                    Toolbox._scrollY = 0
                end
            end
        end

        local rows = math.ceil(#Constants.ASSET_CATEGORIES / cols)
        curY = curY + rows * (cellH + gap) + 8

        -- Hierarchy containers quick create
        if curY + 22 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
            local halfW = math.floor((innerW - 4) * 0.5)
            if Widgets.button(ui, "tbx_new_group", bx + pad, curY, halfW, 22, "+ Group", {
                icon = "layers",
                tooltip = "Create a Group node in the Scene Hierarchy",
            }) then
                actions.spawnGroup()
            end
            if Widgets.button(ui, "tbx_new_folder", bx + pad + halfW + 4, curY, innerW - halfW - 4, 22, "+ Folder", {
                icon = "layers",
                tooltip = "Create a Folder node in the Scene Hierarchy",
            }) then
                actions.spawnFolder()
            end
        end
        curY = curY + 28

        if curY >= by and curY <= visBottom then
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.border)
            render.drawLine(bx + pad, curY, bx + pad + innerW, curY)
        end
        curY = curY + 8

        local q = string.lower(string.trim(state.toolboxSearch or ""))

        if state.activeToolboxCategory == "models" then
            -- Custom mounted .mdl path spawner
            if curY + 15 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
                Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textSecondary)
                render.drawSimpleText(bx + pad, curY, "Custom Mounted Model (.mdl)", TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.TOP)
            end
            curY = curY + 15

            local customGetter = function() return state.customModelPath end
            local customSetter = function(v) state.customModelPath = v end
            if curY + 24 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                Widgets.textBox(
                    ui,
                    "tbx_custom_mdl",
                    bx + pad,
                    curY,
                    innerW,
                    24,
                    customGetter,
                    customSetter,
                    "models/...",
                    "Enter any mounted GMod model path (e.g. models/props_c17/oildrum001.mdl)"
                )
            else
                ui:registerTextInput("tbx_custom_mdl", customGetter, customSetter)
            end
            curY = curY + 28

            local cpath = state.customModelPath or ""
            if cpath ~= Toolbox._lastCustomPath then
                Toolbox._lastCustomPath = cpath
                Toolbox._lastCustomOk = Utils.checkModelMounted(cpath)
                local fname = string.getFileFromFilename(cpath) or "Model"
                local cleanName = string.stripExtension(fname)
                Toolbox._lastCustomName = (cleanName ~= "" and cleanName or "Model")
            end

            if curY + 24 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                local customOk = Toolbox._lastCustomOk
                local spawnLabel = customOk and "Spawn Custom Model" or "Model Path Not Found"
                if Widgets.button(ui, "tbx_spawn_custom", bx + pad, curY, innerW, 24, spawnLabel, {
                    icon = "cube",
                    disabled = not customOk,
                    tooltip = customOk and ("Spawn " .. cpath .. " in front of camera") or "Verify path starts with models/ and exists in GMod",
                }) then
                    actions.spawnQuickModel(Toolbox._lastCustomName, cpath)
                end
            end
            curY = curY + 32

            if curY + 15 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
                Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textSecondary)
                render.drawSimpleText(bx + pad, curY, "Starter Models (Click to Spawn)", TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.TOP)
            end
            curY = curY + 16

            local list = getFilteredModels(q)
            if #list > 0 then
                for _, entry in ipairs(list) do
                    if curY + 24 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                        if Widgets.button(ui, entry.id, bx + pad, curY, innerW, 24, entry.name, {
                            icon = "cube",
                            subtle = true,
                            tooltip = entry.tip,
                        }) then
                            actions.spawnQuickModel(entry.name, entry.model)
                        end
                    end
                    curY = curY + 26
                end
            else
                if curY + 16 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
                    Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textMuted)
                    render.drawSimpleText(bx + pad, curY, "No starter models match filter.", TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.TOP)
                end
                curY = curY + 20
            end

            curY = curY + 6
            local nh = 64
            if curY + nh >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                nh = Widgets.phaseNotice(
                    bx + pad, curY, innerW,
                    "Full Asset Scanner",
                    { "Auto-indexing all GMod/addon", "models arrives in Phase 6." },
                    "Phase 6"
                )
            end
            curY = curY + nh + 8

        elseif state.activeToolboxCategory == "sprites" then
            if curY + 15 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
                Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textSecondary)
                render.drawSimpleText(bx + pad, curY, "Starter Sprites (Click to Spawn)", TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.TOP)
            end
            curY = curY + 16

            local list = getFilteredSprites(q)
            for _, entry in ipairs(list) do
                if curY + 24 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    if Widgets.button(ui, entry.id, bx + pad, curY, innerW, 24, entry.name, {
                        icon = "sparkle",
                        subtle = true,
                        tooltip = entry.tip,
                    }) then
                        actions.spawnSprite(entry.name, entry.material)
                    end
                end
                curY = curY + 26
            end

            curY = curY + 6
            local nh = 64
            if curY + nh >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                nh = Widgets.phaseNotice(
                    bx + pad, curY, innerW,
                    "Dedicated Sprite Editor",
                    { "Size/fade/lifetime curves & material", "browser arrive in Phase 7." },
                    "Phase 7"
                )
            end
            curY = curY + nh + 8
        else
            local nh = Widgets.phaseNotice(
                bx + pad,
                curY,
                innerW,
                activeCatInfo.label,
                {
                    activeCatInfo.desc,
                    "",
                    "Use the Models or Sprites tabs above",
                    "to spawn objects in Phase 2.",
                },
                "Upcoming"
            )
            curY = curY + nh + 8
        end

        Toolbox._contentH = (curY - startY) + pad
        maxScroll = math.max(0, Toolbox._contentH - bh)

        if maxScroll > 0 then
            Toolbox._scrollY = Widgets.scrollbar(
                ui, "tbx_sb",
                bx + bw - 8, by + 2, 6, bh - 4,
                Toolbox._scrollY, Toolbox._contentH, bh
            )
        end

        ui:popClip()
    end

    SOLACE.Toolbox = Toolbox

    -- Reserved for Phase 6: Incremental file.findInGame mounted asset scanner
    SOLACE.AssetScanner = { ready = false, phase = 6 }
end)(SOLACE) end

-- ============================================================================
-- SECTION 17.5: Floating Dear ImGui windows & undocked panel hosts
-- ============================================================================
do (function(SOLACE)
    local Theme    = SOLACE.Theme
    local ImGui    = SOLACE.ImGui
    local Toolbox  = SOLACE.Toolbox
    local Explorer = SOLACE.Explorer
    local Properties = SOLACE.Properties
    local Timeline = SOLACE.Timeline

    local Windows = {
        _demo = {
            chk = true,
            radio = 1,
            fval = 42.5,
            ival = 16,
            dval = 3.14,
            vec = { 10, 20, 30 },
            comboIdx = 1,
            text = "Hello ImGui!",
            progress = 0.68,
            selIdx = 2,
            clickCount = 0,
        },
        _consoleFilter = "",
        _consoleTab = "All",
    }

    local THEME_KEYS = { "dark", "classic", "light" }
    local THEME_LABELS = { "ImGui Dark", "ImGui Classic", "ImGui Light" }
    local COMBO_PRESETS = { "Default Studio", "Minimal Viewport", "High Contrast", "Cinematic" }
    local SELECTABLE_ITEMS = { "Scene Root", "Cube Hologram", "Key Light", "Camera Rig", "Timeline Track" }

    local function drawDemoWindow(ui, state)
        local d = Windows._demo
        local vis, open = ImGui.Begin("win_demo", "Dear ImGui Widget Showcase", state.windows.demo, {
            x = 290, y = 78, w = 390, h = 480, minW = 280, minH = 240,
        })
        if not open then
            state.windows.demo = false
        end
        if not vis then
            if open then ImGui.End() end
            return
        end

        ImGui.TextColored(Theme.C.accentBright, "SOLACE.ImGui Native Immediate-Mode UI")
        ImGui.TextDisabled("Drag titlebar, resize bottom-right grip, click to raise Z-order.")
        if ImGui.SmallButton("Open ImGui Theme & Style (Prefs)") then
            state.windows.settings = true
            ImGui.bringToFront("win_settings")
        end
        ImGui.Separator()

        if ImGui.BeginTabBar("demo_tabs") then
            if ImGui.BeginTabItem("Widgets") then
                if ImGui.CollapsingHeader("Buttons & Toggles", true) then
                    if ImGui.Button("Action Button") then
                        d.clickCount = d.clickCount + 1
                        ImGui.Notify("ImGui Button", "Clicked " .. d.clickCount .. " time(s)", "info", 2.4)
                    end
                    ImGui.SameLine()
                    if ImGui.SmallButton("Reset##demo_rst") then
                        d.clickCount = 0
                        d.fval = 42.5
                        d.ival = 16
                    end
                    ImGui.SameLine()
                    ImGui.TextDisabled("Count: " .. d.clickCount)

                    d.chk = ImGui.Checkbox("Enable Feature Checkbox", d.chk)

                    if ImGui.RadioButton("Mode A", d.radio == 1) then d.radio = 1 end
                    ImGui.SameLine()
                    if ImGui.RadioButton("Mode B", d.radio == 2) then d.radio = 2 end
                    ImGui.SameLine()
                    if ImGui.RadioButton("Mode C", d.radio == 3) then d.radio = 3 end
                end

                ImGui.Spacing(4)
                if ImGui.CollapsingHeader("Sliders, Drag & Dropdowns", true) then
                    d.fval = ImGui.SliderFloat("Float Slider", d.fval, 0, 100, "%.1f")
                    d.ival = ImGui.SliderInt("Int Slider", d.ival, 1, 64, "%d")
                    d.dval = ImGui.DragFloat("Drag Float", d.dval, 0.05, -100, 100, "%.2f")
                    local nx, ny, nz = ImGui.DragFloat3("Sample Vec3", d.vec[1], d.vec[2], d.vec[3], 0.5, "%.1f")
                    d.vec[1], d.vec[2], d.vec[3] = nx, ny, nz
                    d.comboIdx = ImGui.Combo("Preset", d.comboIdx, COMBO_PRESETS, 6)
                    d.text = ImGui.InputText("Text Input", d.text, "Type text...")
                    d.progress = ImGui.SliderFloat("Progress", d.progress, 0, 1, "%.2f")
                    ImGui.ProgressBar(d.progress, 0, 18, string.format("Bake %d%%", math.floor(d.progress * 100 + 0.5)))
                end
                ImGui.EndTabItem()
            end

            if ImGui.BeginTabItem("Trees & Lists") then
                ImGui.Text("Hierarchical TreeNode Demo:")
                if ImGui.TreeNode("Studio Scene Root", true) then
                    ImGui.BulletText("Hologram Layer (active)")
                    if ImGui.TreeNode("Lighting Rig", false) then
                        ImGui.BulletText("Key Light (45 deg)")
                        ImGui.BulletText("Rim Light (cool)")
                        ImGui.TreePop()
                    end
                    ImGui.TreePop()
                end
                ImGui.Separator()
                ImGui.Text("Scrollable Child Selectables:")
                if ImGui.BeginChild("demo_list_child", 0, 130, true) then
                    for idx, name in ipairs(SELECTABLE_ITEMS) do
                        if ImGui.Selectable(name .. "##sel_" .. idx, d.selIdx == idx) then
                            d.selIdx = idx
                        end
                    end
                    ImGui.EndChild()
                end
                ImGui.EndTabItem()
            end

            if ImGui.BeginTabItem("Notifications") then
                ImGui.TextWrapped("Trigger Dear ImGui toast notifications stacked cleanly over the viewport:")
                ImGui.Spacing(4)
                if ImGui.Button("Info Toast", 130) then
                    ImGui.Notify("Info Notice", "Viewport grid updated cleanly.", "info", 3.2)
                end
                ImGui.SameLine()
                if ImGui.Button("Success Toast", 130) then
                    ImGui.Notify("Operation Complete", "Scene node spawned in 3D space.", "success", 3.2)
                end
                if ImGui.Button("Warning Toast", 130) then
                    ImGui.Notify("Quota Advisory", "CPU budget at 65% threshold.", "warning", 3.5)
                end
                ImGui.SameLine()
                if ImGui.Button("Error Toast", 130) then
                    ImGui.Notify("Validation Error", "Sample error notification test.", "error", 3.5)
                end
                ImGui.EndTabItem()
            end

            ImGui.EndTabBar()
        end

        ImGui.End()
    end

    local function drawSettingsWindow(ui, state, layout)
        local vis, open = ImGui.Begin("win_settings", "Studio & ImGui Settings", state.windows.settings, {
            x = 700, y = 78, w = 360, h = 440, minW = 260, minH = 220,
        })
        if not open then
            state.windows.settings = false
        end
        if not vis then
            if open then ImGui.End() end
            return
        end

        if ImGui.CollapsingHeader("Appearance & Dear ImGui Style", true) then
            local curThemeIdx = 1
            for i, k in ipairs(THEME_KEYS) do
                if k == state.uiTheme then curThemeIdx = i break end
            end
            local newThemeIdx, themeChanged = ImGui.Combo("Theme", curThemeIdx, THEME_LABELS, 4)
            if themeChanged then
                state.uiTheme = THEME_KEYS[newThemeIdx] or "dark"
                Theme.applyTheme(state.uiTheme, state.uiRounding)
                ImGui.Notify("Theme Changed", "Applied " .. (THEME_LABELS[newThemeIdx] or "ImGui Dark"), "success", 2.5)
            end

            local newRound, roundChanged = ImGui.SliderInt("Rounding", state.uiRounding or 4, 0, 8, "%d px")
            if roundChanged then
                state.uiRounding = newRound
                Theme.applyTheme(state.uiTheme, state.uiRounding)
            end
        end

        ImGui.Spacing(4)
        if ImGui.CollapsingHeader("Workspace Panels & Undocking", true) then
            local p = state.panels
            local fp = state.floatingPanels
            p.toolbox = ImGui.Checkbox("Show Toolbox", p.toolbox)
            ImGui.SameLine(180)
            fp.toolbox = ImGui.Checkbox("Float##flt_tbx", fp.toolbox)

            p.explorer = ImGui.Checkbox("Show Explorer", p.explorer)
            ImGui.SameLine(180)
            fp.explorer = ImGui.Checkbox("Float##flt_exp", fp.explorer)

            p.properties = ImGui.Checkbox("Show Properties", p.properties)
            ImGui.SameLine(180)
            fp.properties = ImGui.Checkbox("Float##flt_prop", fp.properties)

            p.timeline = ImGui.Checkbox("Show Timeline", p.timeline)
            ImGui.SameLine(180)
            fp.timeline = ImGui.Checkbox("Float##flt_tl", fp.timeline)

            if ImGui.Button("Dock All & Reset Layout", 200) then
                fp.toolbox = false
                fp.explorer = false
                fp.properties = false
                fp.timeline = false
                layout:reset()
                ImGui.Notify("Layout Restored", "All panels docked to default layout", "info", 2.5)
            end
        end

        ImGui.Spacing(4)
        if ImGui.CollapsingHeader("Freecam & Viewport", true) then
            local cam = state.camera
            cam.speed = ImGui.SliderInt("Cam Speed", cam.speed, 40, 2000, "%d u/s")
            cam.fov = ImGui.SliderInt("Field of View", cam.fov, 45, 110, "%d deg")
            cam.sensitivity = ImGui.SliderFloat("Mouse Sens", cam.sensitivity, 0.04, 0.50, "%.2f")
            local g = state.grid
            g.enabled = ImGui.Checkbox("Show 3D Ground Grid", g.enabled)
            g.showAxes = ImGui.Checkbox("Show 3D Origin Axes", g.showAxes)
            g.showTelemetry = ImGui.Checkbox("Show Telemetry HUD", g.showTelemetry)
        end

        ImGui.End()
    end

    local function drawConsoleWindow(ui, state, events)
        local vis, open = ImGui.Begin("win_console", "Console & Event Log", state.windows.console, {
            x = 290, y = 576, w = 520, h = 230, minW = 300, minH = 150,
        })
        if not open then
            state.windows.console = false
        end
        if not vis then
            if open then ImGui.End() end
            return
        end

        Windows._consoleFilter = ImGui.InputText("Filter", Windows._consoleFilter, "Filter events...")
        if ImGui.SmallButton("Clear Log") then
            events:clearLogs()
        end
        ImGui.SameLine()
        if ImGui.SmallButton("Emit Test Event") then
            events:log("info", "Manual diagnostic checkpoint logged")
            ImGui.Notify("Console", "Checkpoint added to Event Log", "info", 2.0)
        end

        if ImGui.BeginTabBar("console_tabs") then
            if ImGui.BeginTabItem("All") then Windows._consoleTab = "All"; ImGui.EndTabItem() end
            if ImGui.BeginTabItem("Info") then Windows._consoleTab = "info"; ImGui.EndTabItem() end
            if ImGui.BeginTabItem("Warn/Err") then Windows._consoleTab = "warn"; ImGui.EndTabItem() end
            ImGui.EndTabBar()
        end

        if ImGui.BeginChild("console_body", 0, 0, true) then
            local logs = events:getLogs()
            local q = string.lower(Windows._consoleFilter or "")
            local tab = Windows._consoleTab
            for i = #logs, 1, -1 do
                local e = logs[i]
                local matchTab = (tab == "All") or (tab == "info" and e.level == "info") or (tab == "warn" and e.level ~= "info")
                local matchText = (q == "") or (string.find(string.lower(e.text), q, 1, true) ~= nil)
                if matchTab and matchText then
                    local col = Theme.C.textSecondary
                    if e.level == "warning" then col = Theme.C.warning
                    elseif e.level == "error" then col = Theme.C.danger end
                    ImGui.TextColored(col, string.format("[%.1fs] %s", e.time or 0, e.text))
                end
            end
            ImGui.EndChild()
        end

        ImGui.End()
    end

    local function drawFloatingPanel(id, title, key, defaultRect, state, drawInner)
        if not state.panels[key] or not state.floatingPanels[key] then return end
        local vis, open = ImGui.Begin(id, title, true, {
            x = defaultRect.x,
            y = defaultRect.y,
            w = defaultRect.w,
            h = defaultRect.h,
            minW = defaultRect.minW or 200,
            minH = defaultRect.minH or 140,
            onDock = function()
                state.floatingPanels[key] = false
                ImGui.Notify("Panel Docked", title .. " docked back into workspace", "info", 2.0)
            end,
        })
        if not open then
            state.panels[key] = false
        end
        if vis then
            local win = ImGui.getWindow(id)
            local titleH = Theme.Style.titleBarH or 24
            local innerRect = {
                x = win.x + 1,
                y = win.y + titleH + 1,
                w = math.max(0, win.w - 2),
                h = math.max(0, win.h - titleH - 2),
            }
            drawInner(innerRect)
        end
        if open then
            ImGui.End()
        end
    end

    local WINDOW_SPECS = {
        { id = "win_panel_toolbox", isOpen = function(s) return s.panels.toolbox and s.floatingPanels.toolbox end,
          draw = function(ui, s, _, _, _, _, _, actions)
              drawFloatingPanel("win_panel_toolbox", "Toolbox", "toolbox", { x = 24, y = 68, w = 250, h = 480 }, s, function(r)
                  Toolbox.draw(ui, s, r, actions, { hideFrame = true })
              end)
          end },
        { id = "win_panel_explorer", isOpen = function(s) return s.panels.explorer and s.floatingPanels.explorer end,
          draw = function(ui, s, _, sg, _, _, _, actions)
              drawFloatingPanel("win_panel_explorer", "Explorer", "explorer", { x = 1040, y = 68, w = 280, h = 320 }, s, function(r)
                  Explorer.draw(ui, s, sg, r, actions, { hideFrame = true })
              end)
          end },
        { id = "win_panel_properties", isOpen = function(s) return s.panels.properties and s.floatingPanels.properties end,
          draw = function(ui, s, _, sg, rt, cam, _, actions)
              drawFloatingPanel("win_panel_properties", "Properties", "properties", { x = 1040, y = 400, w = 290, h = 380 }, s, function(r)
                  Properties.draw(ui, s, sg, rt, cam, r, actions, { hideFrame = true })
              end)
          end },
        { id = "win_panel_timeline", isOpen = function(s) return s.panels.timeline and s.floatingPanels.timeline end,
          draw = function(ui, s, _, sg)
              drawFloatingPanel("win_panel_timeline", "Timeline", "timeline", { x = 120, y = 640, w = 860, h = 200, minW = 360 }, s, function(r)
                  Timeline.draw(ui, s, sg, r, { hideFrame = true })
              end)
          end },
        { id = "win_demo", isOpen = function(s) return s.windows and s.windows.demo end,
          draw = function(ui, s) drawDemoWindow(ui, s) end },
        { id = "win_settings", isOpen = function(s) return s.windows and s.windows.settings end,
          draw = function(ui, s, layout) drawSettingsWindow(ui, s, layout) end },
        { id = "win_console", isOpen = function(s) return s.windows and s.windows.console end,
          draw = function(ui, s, _, _, _, _, events) drawConsoleWindow(ui, s, events) end },
    }

    local SPEC_BY_ID = {}
    for _, sp in ipairs(WINDOW_SPECS) do
        SPEC_BY_ID[sp.id] = sp
    end

    function Windows.drawAll(ui, state, layout, sceneGraph, runtime, camera, events, actions)
        local drawn = {}
        local z = ImGui._zOrder
        -- Snapshot Z-order so click-to-front mid-frame doesn't skip or double-draw a window
        local orderCopy = {}
        for i = 1, #z do
            orderCopy[i] = z[i]
        end

        for i = 1, #orderCopy do
            local id = orderCopy[i]
            local sp = SPEC_BY_ID[id]
            if sp and sp.isOpen(state) then
                sp.draw(ui, state, layout, sceneGraph, runtime, camera, events, actions)
                drawn[id] = true
            end
        end

        -- Draw any newly opened windows not yet in _zOrder
        for _, sp in ipairs(WINDOW_SPECS) do
            if not drawn[sp.id] and sp.isOpen(state) then
                sp.draw(ui, state, layout, sceneGraph, runtime, camera, events, actions)
            end
        end
    end

    SOLACE.Windows = Windows
end)(SOLACE) end

-- ============================================================================
-- SECTION 18: Project serialization (Phase 12)
-- ============================================================================
do (function(SOLACE)
    -- Reserved for Phase 12: JSON Project Save/Load & Reusable Presets in sf_filedata/solace/.
    local ProjectStorage = {
        ready = false,
        phase = 12,
    }

    SOLACE.ProjectStorage = ProjectStorage
end)(SOLACE) end

-- ============================================================================
-- SECTION 19: StarfallEx Lua exporter (Phase 13)
-- ============================================================================
do (function(SOLACE)
    -- Reserved for Phase 13: AI-Friendly IR & Clean StarfallEx Lua Code Exporter.
    local LuaExporter = {
        ready = false,
        phase = 13,
    }

    SOLACE.LuaExporter = LuaExporter
end)(SOLACE) end

-- ============================================================================
-- SECTION 20: Main execution loop
-- ============================================================================
do (function(SOLACE)
    local Constants    = SOLACE.Constants
    local Events       = SOLACE.Events
    local Commands     = SOLACE.Commands
    local SceneGraph   = SOLACE.SceneGraph
    local SceneRuntime = SOLACE.SceneRuntime
    local Theme        = SOLACE.Theme
    local UIManager    = SOLACE.UIManager
    local Layout       = SOLACE.Layout
    local ViewportGrid = SOLACE.ViewportGrid
    local Freecam      = SOLACE.Freecam
    local Selection    = SOLACE.Selection
    local Gizmos       = SOLACE.Gizmos
    local Toolbar      = SOLACE.Toolbar
    local Toolbox      = SOLACE.Toolbox
    local Explorer     = SOLACE.Explorer
    local Properties   = SOLACE.Properties
    local Timeline     = SOLACE.Timeline
    local ImGui        = SOLACE.ImGui
    local Windows      = SOLACE.Windows




    Theme.initFonts()

    local events     = Events.new()
    local commands   = Commands.new(events, 100)
    local sceneGraph = SceneGraph.new(events)
    local runtime    = SceneRuntime.new(sceneGraph, events)
    local state      = Constants.createDefaultState()
    local ui         = UIManager.new(state)
    local layout     = Layout.new(state)
    local camera     = Freecam.new(state)

    local hudConnected = false
    local lastFrameTime = timer.realtime()
    local fpsSmooth = 60

    local HIDDEN_HUD_ELEMENTS = {
        CHudHealth = true,
        CHudBattery = true,
        CHudAmmo = true,
        CHudSecondaryAmmo = true,
        CHudCrosshair = true,
        CHudWeaponSelection = true,
        CHudDamageIndicator = true,
        CHudSuitPower = true,
        CHudHintDisplay = true,
        CHudZoom = true,
        CHudPoisonDamageIndicator = true,
        CHudSquadStatus = true,
    }

    local f6Down = false
    local drawErrorLogged = false
    local firstDrawLogged = false

    local function isHudActiveSafe()
        if type(render.isHUDActive) == "function" then
            return render.isHUDActive()
        end
        return hasPermission("render.hud")
    end

    local function formatDiagState(prefix)
        return string.format(
            "%s | studioOpen=%s | hudActive=%s | hudConn=%s | perms(hud=%s, calcview=%s, cursor=%s, input=%s, holo=%s)",
            prefix,
            tostring(state.studioOpen),
            tostring(isHudActiveSafe()),
            tostring(hudConnected),
            tostring(hasPermission("render.hud")),
            tostring(hasPermission("render.calcview")),
            tostring(hasPermission("input.enablecursor")),
            tostring(hasPermission("input")),
            tostring(hasPermission("hologram.create"))
        )
    end

    local lowCpuUntil = 0

    local function isOverlayOrUnfocused()
        if game.hasFocus and not game.hasFocus() then return true end
        if game.isPauseMenuVisible and game.isPauseMenuVisible() then return true end
        return false
    end

    local function getCpuRatio()
        local avgFn = cpuAverage or quotaAverage
        local maxFn = cpuMax or quotaMax
        if not avgFn or not maxFn then return 0 end
        local cap = maxFn()
        if cap <= 0 then return 0 end
        local ratio = avgFn() / cap
        if cpuUsed then
            local usedRatio = cpuUsed() / cap
            if usedRatio > ratio then ratio = usedRatio end
        end
        return ratio
    end

    local function syncCursor()
        if isOverlayOrUnfocused() then return end
        if not hasPermission("input.enablecursor") then return end
        local wantCursor = hudConnected and state.studioOpen and (not state.viewportLooking)
        if hasPermission("input") then
            if input.getCursorVisible() ~= wantCursor then
                input.enableCursor(wantCursor)
            end
        else
            input.enableCursor(wantCursor)
        end
    end

    local function setStudioOpen(open)
        state.studioOpen = open
        if not open then
            if Gizmos.dragging then
                Gizmos.finishDrag(sceneGraph, commands)
            end
            state.viewportLooking = false
            ui.focusedInputId = nil
            ui.activeId = nil
            ui:cancelAutoScroll()
        elseif not camera.initialized then
            camera:initFromPlayer()
        end
        syncCursor()
    end

    local function toggleStudio()
        local nextOpen = not state.studioOpen
        if nextOpen and not isHudActiveSafe() and isValid(player()) and player() == owner() and hasPermission("enablehud") then
            pcall(enableHud, nil, true)
        end
        if isHudActiveSafe() then
            hudConnected = true
        elseif nextOpen and not hasPermission("render.hud") then
            print("[SOLACE] HUD not connected yet — press E (Use) on the Starfall chip or link a Starfall HUD component.")
        end
        setStudioOpen(nextOpen)
        printConsole(formatDiagState(nextOpen and "[SOLACE] Studio opened (F6)" or "[SOLACE] Studio minimized (F6)"))
    end

    local function resolveOrigin()
        ViewportGrid.resolveOrigin(state)
        sceneGraph:setOrigin(state.grid.originPos)
    end

    local function getTargetContainerParentId()
        local sel = sceneGraph:getSelectedNode()
        if sel and (sel.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.GROUP or sel.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.FOLDER) then
            return sel.id
        end
        return sceneGraph:getRootId()
    end

    local actions = {}
    actions.hasSelection = false
    actions.canUndo = false
    actions.canRedo = false
    actions.undoLabel = nil
    actions.redoLabel = nil

    function actions.toggleStudio(open)
        setStudioOpen(open)
    end

    function actions.setTool(toolId)
        if state.activeTool == toolId then return end
        if Gizmos.dragging then
            Gizmos.finishDrag(sceneGraph, commands)
            ui:release("gizmo_drag")
        end
        state.activeTool = toolId
    end

    function actions.undo()
        if Gizmos.dragging then
            Gizmos.cancelDrag(sceneGraph)
            ui:release("gizmo_drag")
        end
        return commands:undo()
    end

    function actions.redo()
        if Gizmos.dragging then
            Gizmos.cancelDrag(sceneGraph)
            ui:release("gizmo_drag")
        end
        return commands:redo()
    end

    function actions.resolveOrigin()
        resolveOrigin()
    end

    function actions.focusSelected()
        camera:focusNode(sceneGraph, runtime)
    end

    function actions.spawnQuickModel(name, modelPath)
        local posTbl, angTbl = camera:getSpawnTransform(state.spawnDistance)
        local parentId = getTargetContainerParentId()
        local node = sceneGraph:createModel(name, modelPath, posTbl, angTbl, parentId)
        sceneGraph:setSelectedId(node.id)
    end

    function actions.spawnSprite(name, matPath)
        local posTbl = camera:getSpawnTransform(state.spawnDistance)
        local parentId = getTargetContainerParentId()
        local node = sceneGraph:createSprite(name, matPath, posTbl, parentId)
        sceneGraph:setSelectedId(node.id)
    end

    function actions.spawnGroup()
        local posTbl = camera:getSpawnTransform(state.spawnDistance)
        local parentId = getTargetContainerParentId()
        local node = sceneGraph:createGroup("Group", posTbl, parentId)
        sceneGraph:setSelectedId(node.id)
    end

    function actions.spawnFolder()
        local parentId = getTargetContainerParentId()
        local node = sceneGraph:createFolder("Folder", parentId)
        sceneGraph:setSelectedId(node.id)
    end

    function actions.deleteSelected()
        local ids = {}
        for _, id in ipairs(sceneGraph:getSelectedIds()) do
            if id ~= sceneGraph:getRootId() then
                ids[#ids + 1] = id
            end
        end
        for _, id in ipairs(ids) do
            sceneGraph:removeNode(id)
        end
    end

    local startupMsg = formatDiagState("[SOLACE CL] Loaded")
    print(startupMsg)
    printConsole(startupMsg)

    timer.simple(0.05, function()
        resolveOrigin()
        camera:initFromPlayer()
        if not isHudActiveSafe() and isValid(player()) and player() == owner() and hasPermission("enablehud") then
            pcall(enableHud, nil, true)
        end
        if isHudActiveSafe() then
            hudConnected = true
            syncCursor()
        end
        printConsole(formatDiagState("[SOLACE CL] Post-init"))
    end)

    timer.simple(0.35, function()
        if not isHudActiveSafe() and not hudConnected then
            print("[SOLACE] Waiting for HUD connection — press E (Use) on the Starfall chip or link a Starfall HUD component, then press F6.")
        end
    end)

    hook.add("StarfallUsed", "solace_cl_used", function(activator, used)
        if used ~= chip() or activator ~= player() then return end
        if not isHudActiveSafe() and hasPermission("enablehud") then
            pcall(enableHud, nil, true)
        end
        if isHudActiveSafe() then
            hudConnected = true
        end
        setStudioOpen(true)
        local msg = formatDiagState("[SOLACE CL] Chip used (E)")
        print(msg)
        printConsole(msg)
    end)

    hook.add("HUDConnected", "solace_hud_connected", function(ent, ply)
        if ply == player() then
            hudConnected = true
            if not camera.initialized then
                camera:initFromPlayer()
            end
            syncCursor()
            printConsole(formatDiagState("[SOLACE CL] HUDConnected"))
        end
    end)

    hook.add("HUDDisconnected", "solace_hud_disconnected", function(ent, ply)
        if ply == player() then
            hudConnected = false
            if Gizmos.dragging then
                Gizmos.finishDrag(sceneGraph, commands)
            end
            state.viewportLooking = false
            ui.focusedInputId = nil
            ui.activeId = nil
            if hasPermission("input.enablecursor") then
                input.enableCursor(false)
            end
        end
    end)

    hook.add("HUDShouldDraw", "solace_hide_game_hud", function(name)
        if state.studioOpen and HIDDEN_HUD_ELEMENTS[name] then
            return false
        end
    end)

    hook.add("CalcView", "solace_freecam_view", function(origin, angles, fov, znear, zfar)
        if not state.studioOpen or not hudConnected then return end
        if not hasPermission("render.calcview") then return end
        if isOverlayOrUnfocused() or getCpuRatio() > 0.72 then
            return camera._viewOut.origin and camera._viewOut or nil
        end
        return camera:calcView(origin, angles, fov, znear, zfar)
    end)

    hook.add("MouseMoved", "solace_freecam_look", function(dx, dy)
        if state.studioOpen and state.viewportLooking then
            camera:onMouseMoved(dx, dy)
        end
    end)

    hook.add("Think", "solace_runtime_flush", function()
        runtime:flush()
        if hasPermission("input") then
            local isF6 = input.isKeyDown(KEY.F6)
            if isF6 and not f6Down then
                f6Down = true
                toggleStudio()
            elseif not isF6 and f6Down then
                f6Down = false
            end
        end
    end)

    hook.add("Removed", "solace_cleanup", function()
        runtime:cleanup()
        if hasPermission("input.enablecursor") then
            input.enableCursor(false)
        end
    end)

    hook.add("InputPressed", "solace_input_pressed", function(key)
        if key == KEY.TAB and hasPermission("input") and input.isShiftDown() then
            lowCpuUntil = timer.realtime() + 0.8
            ui:cancelAutoScroll()
            return
        end

        if key == KEY.F6 then
            if not f6Down then
                f6Down = true
                toggleStudio()
            end
            return
        end

        if not state.studioOpen or isOverlayOrUnfocused() then return end

        if key == KEY.ESCAPE and Gizmos.dragging then
            Gizmos.cancelDrag(sceneGraph)
            ui:release("gizmo_drag")
            return
        end

        if key == KEY.ESCAPE and ui.autoScroll then
            ui:cancelAutoScroll()
            return
        end

        if ui:handleKeyPressed(key) then
            return
        end

        local ctrl = hasPermission("input") and input.isControlDown() or false
        local shift = hasPermission("input") and input.isShiftDown() or false
        if ctrl then
            if key == KEY.Z then
                if shift then
                    actions.redo()
                else
                    actions.undo()
                end
                return
            elseif key == KEY.Y then
                actions.redo()
                return
            end
        end

        if key == KEY.G then
            state.grid.enabled = not state.grid.enabled
        elseif key == KEY.Q or key == KEY.KEY1 or key == KEY["1"] then
            actions.setTool("select")
        elseif key == KEY.KEY2 or key == KEY["2"] then
            actions.setTool("move")
        elseif key == KEY.E or key == KEY.KEY3 or key == KEY["3"] then
            actions.setTool("rotate")
        elseif key == KEY.R or key == KEY.KEY4 or key == KEY["4"] then
            actions.setTool("scale")
        elseif key == KEY.F then
            actions.focusSelected()
        elseif key == KEY.DELETE then
            actions.deleteSelected()
        elseif key == KEY.ESCAPE then
            sceneGraph:clearSelection()
        end
    end)

    hook.add("InputReleased", "solace_input_released", function(key)
        if key == KEY.F6 then
            f6Down = false
        end
    end)

    hook.add("InputBindPressed", "solace_block_binds", function(bind)
        if hudConnected and not isOverlayOrUnfocused() and ui:shouldBlockBind(bind) then
            return true
        end
    end)

    hook.add("MouseWheeled", "solace_mouse_wheel", function(delta)
        if not state.studioOpen or isOverlayOrUnfocused() then return end
        if state.viewportLooking then
            camera:onMouseWheelInViewport(delta)
        else
            ui:onMouseWheeled(delta)
        end
    end)

    hook.add("PostDrawOpaqueRenderables", "solace_viewport_3d", function(depth, skybox, skybox3d)
        if skybox or skybox3d then return end
        if not state.studioOpen then return end
        if isOverlayOrUnfocused() or timer.realtime() < lowCpuUntil or getCpuRatio() > 0.62 then return end
        ViewportGrid.draw3D(state)
        Selection.draw3D(state, camera, sceneGraph, runtime)
        Gizmos.draw3D(state, camera, sceneGraph)
    end)

    hook.add("PostDrawTranslucentRenderables", "solace_viewport_sprites", function(depth, skybox, skybox3d)
        if skybox or skybox3d then return end
        if not state.studioOpen then return end
        if isOverlayOrUnfocused() or timer.realtime() < lowCpuUntil or getCpuRatio() > 0.62 then return end
        runtime:drawSprites(state)
    end)

    local rtSupported = (type(render.createRenderTarget) == "function")
        and (type(render.selectRenderTarget) == "function")
        and (type(render.setRenderTargetTexture) == "function")
        and (type(render.drawTexturedRectUV) == "function")

    local panelCaches = {
        toolbar    = { rtName = "sol_rt_tb",   w = 0, h = 0, sig = nil, wasInPanel = false, wasActive = false, lastHotId = nil, lastTooltip = nil, created = false, failed = false, localRect = { x = 0, y = 0, w = 0, h = 0 } },
        toolbox    = { rtName = "sol_rt_tbx",  w = 0, h = 0, sig = nil, wasInPanel = false, wasActive = false, lastHotId = nil, lastTooltip = nil, created = false, failed = false, localRect = { x = 0, y = 0, w = 0, h = 0 } },
        explorer   = { rtName = "sol_rt_exp",  w = 0, h = 0, sig = nil, wasInPanel = false, wasActive = false, lastHotId = nil, lastTooltip = nil, created = false, failed = false, localRect = { x = 0, y = 0, w = 0, h = 0 } },
        properties = { rtName = "sol_rt_prop", w = 0, h = 0, sig = nil, wasInPanel = false, wasActive = false, lastHotId = nil, lastTooltip = nil, created = false, failed = false, localRect = { x = 0, y = 0, w = 0, h = 0 } },
        timeline   = { rtName = "sol_rt_tl",   w = 0, h = 0, sig = nil, wasInPanel = false, wasActive = false, lastHotId = nil, lastTooltip = nil, created = false, failed = false, localRect = { x = 0, y = 0, w = 0, h = 0 } },
    }

    local function drawCachedPanel(key, rect, sig, isActiveInPanel, allowRepaint, drawFn)
        local w, h = rect.w, rect.h
        if w <= 0 or h <= 0 then return end

        local c = panelCaches[key]
        local numStrips = math.ceil(w / 1024)
        local totalRTH = numStrips * (h + 2) - 2
        if not rtSupported or c.failed or h > 1024 or totalRTH > 1024 then
            if allowRepaint then
                drawFn(rect)
            end
            return
        end

        if not c.created then
            if not allowRepaint then return end
            local ok = pcall(render.createRenderTarget, c.rtName)
            if not ok then
                c.failed = true
                drawFn(rect)
                return
            end
            c.created = true
        end

        local inPanel = ui:pointInRect(rect.x, rect.y, w, h) and (ui._topHoverWinId == nil)
        local pointerChanged = (ui.dx ~= 0 or ui.dy ~= 0 or ui.leftPressed or ui.leftReleased or ui.leftDown or ui.middlePressed or ui.wheelDelta ~= 0)
        local dirty = (w ~= c.w) or (h ~= c.h) or (sig ~= c.sig)
            or (inPanel and (pointerChanged or not c.wasInPanel))
            or (not inPanel and c.wasInPanel)
            or isActiveInPanel or c.wasActive

        if not allowRepaint and c.w > 0 then
            dirty = false
        end

        if dirty then
            local realMx, realMy = ui.mx, ui.my
            local prevHot = ui.hotId
            local prevTip = ui._tooltipText
            ui.hotId = nil
            ui._tooltipText = nil

            render.selectRenderTarget(c.rtName)
            Theme.beginFrame()
            if numStrips == 1 then
                ui.mx = realMx - rect.x
                ui.my = realMy - rect.y
                ui._rtOffsetX = rect.x
                ui._rtOffsetY = rect.y
                c.localRect.x = 0
                c.localRect.y = 0
                c.localRect.w = w
                c.localRect.h = h
                drawFn(c.localRect)
            else
                local stripHotId, stripTip = nil, nil
                for s = 0, numStrips - 1 do
                    local offX = s * 1024
                    local offY = s * (h + 2)
                    local stripW = math.min(1024, w - offX)
                    ui.hotId = nil
                    ui._tooltipText = nil
                    ui.mx = realMx - rect.x - offX
                    ui.my = realMy - rect.y + offY
                    ui._rtOffsetX = rect.x + offX
                    ui._rtOffsetY = rect.y - offY
                    c.localRect.x = -offX
                    c.localRect.y = offY
                    c.localRect.w = w
                    c.localRect.h = h
                    ui:pushClip(0, offY, stripW, h)
                    drawFn(c.localRect)
                    ui:popClip()
                    if ui.hotId ~= nil then stripHotId = ui.hotId end
                    if ui._tooltipText ~= nil then stripTip = ui._tooltipText end
                end
                ui.hotId = stripHotId
                ui._tooltipText = stripTip
            end
            ui._rtOffsetX = 0
            ui._rtOffsetY = 0
            ui.mx = realMx
            ui.my = realMy
            render.selectRenderTarget(nil)
            Theme.beginFrame()

            c.lastHotId = inPanel and ui.hotId or nil
            c.lastTooltip = inPanel and ui._tooltipText or nil
            if not c.lastHotId then ui.hotId = prevHot end
            if not c.lastTooltip then ui._tooltipText = prevTip end

            c.w = w
            c.h = h
            c.sig = sig
            c.wasInPanel = inPanel
            c.wasActive = isActiveInPanel
        elseif inPanel and not ui.autoScroll then
            if c.lastHotId then ui.hotId = c.lastHotId end
            if c.lastTooltip then ui:setTooltip(c.lastTooltip) end
        end

        render.setRenderTargetTexture(c.rtName)
        Theme.setRGBA(255, 255, 255, 255)
        if numStrips == 1 then
            render.drawTexturedRectUV(rect.x, rect.y, w, h, 0, 0, w / 1024, h / 1024)
        else
            for s = 0, numStrips - 1 do
                local offX = s * 1024
                local offY = s * (h + 2)
                local stripW = math.min(1024, w - offX)
                render.drawTexturedRectUV(
                    rect.x + offX, rect.y, stripW, h,
                    0, offY / 1024, stripW / 1024, (offY + h) / 1024
                )
            end
        end
        render.setRenderTargetTexture(nil)
    end

    local function updatePanelAutoScroll(rects, dt)
        if ui.middlePressed and not state.viewportLooking and not ui.autoScroll and not ui._autoScrollConsumed then
            if rects.toolbox.w > 0 and ui:pointInRect(rects.toolbox.x, rects.toolbox.y, rects.toolbox.w, rects.toolbox.h)
                and Toolbox._contentH > Toolbox._viewH and Toolbox._viewH > 0 then
                ui:beginAutoScroll("toolbox", false, true)
            elseif rects.explorer.w > 0 and ui:pointInRect(rects.explorer.x, rects.explorer.y, rects.explorer.w, rects.explorer.h)
                and Explorer._contentH > Explorer._viewH and Explorer._viewH > 0 then
                ui:beginAutoScroll("explorer", false, true)
            elseif rects.properties.w > 0 and ui:pointInRect(rects.properties.x, rects.properties.y, rects.properties.w, rects.properties.h)
                and Properties._contentH > Properties._viewH and Properties._viewH > 0 then
                ui:beginAutoScroll("properties", false, true)
            elseif rects.timeline.w > 0 and ui:pointInRect(rects.timeline.x, rects.timeline.y, rects.timeline.w, rects.timeline.h) then
                ui:beginAutoScroll("timeline", true, false)
            end
        end

        local a = ui.autoScroll
        if not a then return end

        local dz = 8
        local dy = ui.my - a.originY
        local dx = ui.mx - a.originX

        if a.canV and math.abs(dy) > dz then
            local eff = (dy > 0) and (dy - dz) or (dy + dz)
            local speed = 3.0 + math.min(7.0, math.abs(eff) * 0.04)
            local deltaScroll = eff * speed * dt
            if a.id == "toolbox" then
                local maxS = math.max(0, Toolbox._contentH - Toolbox._viewH)
                Toolbox._scrollY = math.clamp(Toolbox._scrollY + deltaScroll, 0, maxS)
            elseif a.id == "explorer" then
                local maxS = math.max(0, Explorer._contentH - Explorer._viewH)
                Explorer._scrollY = math.clamp(Explorer._scrollY + deltaScroll, 0, maxS)
            elseif a.id == "properties" then
                local maxS = math.max(0, Properties._contentH - Properties._viewH)
                Properties._scrollY = math.clamp(Properties._scrollY + deltaScroll, 0, maxS)
            end
        end

        if a.canH and math.abs(dx) > dz and a.id == "timeline" then
            local effX = (dx > 0) and (dx - dz) or (dx + dz)
            local speed = 2.5 + math.min(6.0, math.abs(effX) * 0.035)
            local tl = state.timeline
            tl.scrollTime = math.clamp(tl.scrollTime + (effX * speed * dt) / math.max(25, tl.zoom), 0, math.max(0, tl.duration - 1))
        end
    end

    hook.add("DrawHUD", "solace_hud_paint", function()
        if not hasPermission("render.hud") then return end
        hudConnected = true

        local now = timer.realtime()
        local rawDt = now - lastFrameTime
        if rawDt > 0.12 then
            lowCpuUntil = math.max(lowCpuUntil, now + 0.6)
        end
        local dt = math.clamp(rawDt, 0.0001, 0.25)
        lastFrameTime = now
        local instFps = 1 / dt
        fpsSmooth = fpsSmooth * 0.92 + instFps * 0.08

        if not firstDrawLogged then
            firstDrawLogged = true
            local sw, sh = render.getResolution()
            printConsole(string.format("[SOLACE CL] DrawHUD active (%dx%d, studioOpen=%s)", sw or 0, sh or 0, tostring(state.studioOpen)))
        end

        local cpuRatio = getCpuRatio()
        if cpuRatio > 0.78 or isOverlayOrUnfocused() then
            if Gizmos.dragging then
                Gizmos.finishDrag(sceneGraph, commands)
                ui:release("gizmo_drag")
            end
            return
        end
        local overlayPaused = (now < lowCpuUntil)

        Theme.beginFrame()

        if not state.studioOpen then
            if overlayPaused then return end
            local scrW = render.getResolution()
            local label = "SOLACE Minimized  |  Press F6 to Open"
            Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
            local tw = render.getTextSize(label)
            local pw = tw + 18
            local px = scrW - pw - 14
            local py = 14
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.hudPill)
            render.drawRectFast(px, py, pw, 24)
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.borderLight)
            render.drawRectOutline(px, py, pw, 24, 1)
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textPrimary)
            render.drawSimpleText(px + 9, py + 12, label, TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)
            return
        end

        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            syncCursor()
            ui:beginFrame(dt)
            ImGui.beginFrame(ui, state, dt)
            layout:compute(ui.scrW, ui.scrH)

            local rects = layout.rects
            if not overlayPaused then
                ViewportGrid.updateLookMode(state, ui, rects.viewport)
                camera:update(dt, ui, rects.viewport)
                updatePanelAutoScroll(rects, dt)
            end

            local sel = sceneGraph:getSelectedNode()
            local selIds = sceneGraph:getSelectedIds()
            actions.hasSelection = (sel ~= nil and sel.id ~= sceneGraph:getRootId())
            actions.canUndo = commands:canUndo()
            actions.canRedo = commands:canRedo()
            actions.undoLabel = commands:getUndoLabel()
            actions.redoLabel = commands:getRedoLabel()

            local actId = ui.activeId
            local focId = ui.focusedInputId
            local blinkPhase = math.floor(ui._frameTime * 2)
            local actPrefix3 = actId and string.sub(actId, 1, 3) or ""
            local actPrefix4 = actId and string.sub(actId, 1, 4) or ""
            local allowRepaint = (not overlayPaused) and (cpuRatio < 0.60)
            local tv = Theme._version or 1

            -- Draw docked studio panels around the live center 3D viewport cutout (1:1 RT cached when idle)
            local winFlags = state.windows and (tostring(state.windows.demo) .. tostring(state.windows.settings) .. tostring(state.windows.console)) or ""
            local fltFlags = state.floatingPanels and (tostring(state.floatingPanels.toolbox) .. tostring(state.floatingPanels.explorer) .. tostring(state.floatingPanels.properties) .. tostring(state.floatingPanels.timeline)) or ""
            local tbSig = tv .. "|" .. state.activeTool .. ":" .. tostring(actions.hasSelection)
                .. ":" .. tostring(commands._version or 1)
                .. ":" .. tostring(state.grid.enabled) .. tostring(state.grid.showAxes) .. tostring(state.grid.showTelemetry)
                .. ":" .. tostring(state.panels.toolbox) .. tostring(state.panels.explorer) .. tostring(state.panels.properties) .. tostring(state.panels.timeline)
                .. ":" .. winFlags .. ":" .. fltFlags
            drawCachedPanel("toolbar", rects.toolbar, tbSig, actPrefix3 == "tb_", allowRepaint, function(r)
                Toolbar.draw(ui, state, layout, r, actions)
            end)
